{"parentUuid":"33ef957a-f999-466a-ab0e-6087518a2544","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"616419db-f8ea-4cbf-8b1b-8e3d3d602562","agentId":"a7319d0ecd9725385","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01K9Th1MjPuwYctgWWmVVv5v","type":"tool_result","content":"     1→---\n     2→type: lore\n     3→title: \"Master Timeline\"\n     4→---\n     5→\n     6→# Master Timeline of Mardov\n     7→\n     8→## Ancient History\n     9→\n    10→- [[Vecna]] and [[Sabine Ra]] — original twin souls, divine jealousy begins\n    11→- [[Sabine Ra|Sabine]] founds Circle of the Murder ([[Mordred|Ramses]]) + [[Artificer's Guild]] ([[Xerxes]])\n    12→- [[Mordred|Ramses]] forces lichdom on [[Sabine Ra|Sabine]]; she crystallizes as Demi-Lich in a phylactery\n    13→- [[Tygras]] makes a deal with an arch-fey from [[Vecna]]'s plane; creates a weapon to bridge worlds\n    14→- [[Ranar Surefoot]] steals the [[Diadem of Dawn]] during the weapon's use; the weapon backfires\n    15→- [[Tygras]]'s superweapon destroys [[uma]], creating the desert; [[Tygras]] sealed in [[Harvest's Zenith]]\n    16→- The [[Matrim Pact]] formed: human sailors, gnomes of [[Fae'ish]], and [[Qual'Xar]] half-elves\n    17→- Covenants of Magic established after [[Tygras]]'s destruction — no being except the Alabaster Seat may possess such power again\n    18→- [[Kolyma]] (Last Curator) establishes Zodiac guardianship of the Zeniths\n    19→- The [[forgesworn]] order established; [[Thorin the Bronze|Thorin Bronzebeard]] builds the original Warforged army\n    20→\n    21→## Recent Past (Decades Before Campaign)\n    22→\n    23→- ~26 years ago: [[Nyx Khamestria|Nyx]] born; parents make a deal with [[Reynardine]] for the philosopher's stone\n    24→- ~8 years ago: Nyx's dominant soul emerges; her sister taken; parents spurn Nyx\n    25→- ~7 years ago: [[Xyn]] born — the \"perfect vessel\" for the arch-fey's plan\n    26→- [[Mordred]] possesses [[Finkle Hollingsworth|Finkle Hollingsworth]] (Gnome, NOT Goblin) at [[Forest's Zenith]]\n    27→- [[Scarlet Tunic Brigade]] founded as a soul-harvesting front\n    28→- [[Thorkon Fireforge]] betrays [[Thorson Bronzebeard II]] at [[Ghost-Eye Thorrin]]'s urging\n    29→- [[Ghost-Eye Thorrin]] betrays [[Thorkon Fireforge|Thorkon]], hunts all [[forgesworn]]\n    30→- [[Thorkon Fireforge|Thorkon]] and [[Hlin Blunderbeat|Hlin]] flee [[Vag'Mahar]]; [[Hlin Blunderbeat|Hlin]] dies giving birth to [[Daina Fireforge|Daina]] and an older brother\n    31→- [[Blood Star]] kills [[Thorson Bronzebeard II]]\n    32→- [[Oblivion Wayne]] deposes her brother, unites all Drow under vampire rule\n    33→- [[Ebon Wayne]] captures [[Zodiac|Bat Zodiac]], discovers [[Spell Plague]]\n    34→- [[Vecna]] raises [[Peraxis Phyrexia|Peraxis Geth]] as Illithilich\n    35→- [[Peraxis Phyrexia]] corrupts the Elder Brain\n    36→- [[Ned'Zailok]] attacks [[Tower Bridge]]; [[Varis Liadon|Varis]]'s father [[Yeldan Liadon|Yeldan]] goes missing\n    37→- [[Scarlet Tunic Brigade]] covers the evacuation; [[Varis Liadon|Varis]] enlists\n    38→- [[Ned'Zailok]] defeated after 18 months of fighting\n    39→\n    40→## Campaign Era (136 Chapters)\n    41→\n    42→### Act 1 (Chapters 0-13) — The Awakening\n    43→\n    44→| Ch. | Event |\n    45→|-----|-------|\n    46→| 0 | Party recruited into [[Scarlet Tunic Brigade]]. [[Jorgen]] arrives pursued by kobolds. Sewer descent. |\n    47→| 1 | [[Dallor Dallorbill|Dallor]] pickpockets note from [[Jorgen]] — proof of setup. |\n    48→| 2 | [[Finkle Hollingsworth|Finkle]] revealed as [[Mordred]]. \"2,000 souls.\" [[Jorgen]] killed, becomes revenant. [[Sabine Ra]] freed from phylactery, sacrifices herself. Crystalline hearts deposited in party. |\n    49→| 3-5 | [[Artificer's Guild]] rescue. [[Xerxes]] exposition. Soul tether. |\n    50→| 6 | Dopplegangers. [[V-Mardov]] rift opens. [[Varis Liadon|Varris]] arrives from alternate reality. |\n    51→| 7-8 | [[Jorgen]] revenant pursues [[Finkle Hollingsworth|Finkle]]. [[Thornberry Shade]] ship. [[jafar]]/Sally intrigue. |\n    52→| 9 | First Prophecy ([[Ruth]]). |\n    53→| 11 | [[Golden Reach]] demons (Danelle/Markus) exposed at Festival of Light. [[Stalwart Little Goldentooth|Stalwart]]'s divine sense. |\n    54→| 12 | Second Prophecy (Hags). Pestilence discovered — brewed from children's souls. |\n    55→| 13 | Third Prophecy ([[Mia]]). [[Mia]] reveals she wiped party's memories. |\n    56→\n    57→### Act 2 (Chapters 14-40) — The Voyage\n    58→\n    59→| Ch. | Event |\n    60→|-----|-------|\n    61→| 17 | [[Nathan Yanagan]] (spy for [[jafar]]) aboard [[Thornberry Shade]]. Tiefling prejudice arc. |\n    62→| 19 | Soul glamours for [[Nyx Khamestria|Nyx]] and [[Tesca Duskhallow|Tesca]]. |\n    63→| 23 | Zodiac spirits revealed — they bet their immortality on the champions. |\n    64→| 24-25 | Tomb of the Last Curator. Four Zenith hieroglyphics. |\n    65→| 26 | [[Kelik Waystar]] revealed as air genasi prince. |\n    66→| 29 | Green Tides of St. Ashbury warning. |\n    67→| 30-31 | [[Peraxis Phyrexia]] revealed. [[Nocturnus Wayne]] kills cousin Dusken, becomes Drider King. |\n    69→| 36 | [[Fen Darkeyes|Devin Darkeyes]] found enslaved by the Aboleth. |\n    70→| 37-38 | Dvinter Castle / Phoenix arc. [[forgesworn]] connections revealed. |\n    71→\n    72→### Act 3 (Chapters 41-86) — The Escalation\n    73→\n    74→| Ch. | Event |\n    75→|-----|-------|\n    76→| 42+ | Campaign splits into parallel tracks. |\n    77→| ~66 | [[Peraxis Phyrexia|Peraxis]]'s Homunculus Thraalm killed. |\n    78→| ~82 | Attempted revival of [[Zodiac|Falak]] the serpent using captured Mahen. |\n    79→| 86 | Reunion. Battle of the Distillery. |\n    80→\n    81→### Act 4 (Chapters 87-136) — The Endgame\n    82→\n    83→| Ch. | Event |\n    84→|-----|-------|\n    85→| 103 | Second Reunion. TPK and revival via myconid network. [[Bergs Fireforge|Bergs]] carrying Kelemtor. |\n    86→| 104-105 | [[Reginald Providence]] killed (kenku in disguise). [[Anchor of War]] silenced. Therzadun thwarted. Silence/Timothy unmasked as Therzadun's vessel. |\n    87→| 106 | Declaration of War on gods. [[Peraxis Phyrexia|Peraxis]] reveals complex motivations — wanted to save the party. |\n    88→| 107 | Ragnarok. Warforged awakening. [[Irethia]] revealed as aasimar created in image of [[Ashara Everstar]]. |\n    89→| 108 | Three races of elves' gods revealed. Casaval reveals [[Irethia]] (prototype), [[Riven Fen|Fen]] (warden), [[Nocturnus Wayne|Nocturnus]] (bane). [[lolth]]'s \"Two Truths and a Lie.\" |\n    90→| 109 | [[Josh Coolbreeze]] cleanses [[Nocturnus Wayne|Nocturnus]] of vampirism. [[lolth]]-[[Raven Queen]] deal revealed. [[Ghost-Eye Thorrin]] disappears. |\n    91→| 113 | [[Brigit Wildfire|Brigitte]] framed for Pestilence \"oil spill.\" [[rimefang]]'s last ride. |\n    92→| 116 | Convergence and Betrayal — all planes converging into the Feywild. |\n    93→| 119 | Time paradox — [[Bergs Fireforge|Bergs]] finds own dead body from 2 weeks ago, kickstarts his own heart. |\n    94→| 133 | [[Reynaldine]]'s true plan revealed: orchestrated everything to ascend to godhood and save Gabriel. Hearthstone exposed as triple agent. |\n    95→| 135 | Book of Shadows plot to open Harvest and Winter Zenith. [[Tiamat]] at Harvest Zenith. |\n    96→| 136 | [[Asmodeus]] killed by [[Josh Coolbreeze]]. Three specters: one into [[Nocturnus Wayne|Nocturnus]]. [[Bergs Fireforge|Bergs]] becomes deity of Mardov's Feywild via Stone Table sacrifice. |\n    97→\n    98→## Prophesied: Gard'Daemon\n    99→\n   100→All four Zeniths held simultaneously. [[V-Mardov|V-MORDRED]]'s invasion repelled. The final configuration of Controllers, Champions, and Anchor Wielders activates.\n   101→\n   102→\n   103→## The True History Of Mardov (Extracted from PPTX)\n   104→\n   105→### Slide 1\n   106→\n   107→The True History Of\n   108→\n   109→---\n   110→\n   111→### Slide 2\n   112→\n   113→Year 0 - The Rose of Creation\n   114→\n   115→The Rose of Creation was the solution to the ever present conflict between Gods of Destruction and Gods of Creation. It is the culmination of countless Gods and Goddesses’ life's work, and is thusly an irresistible target for Destruction. One such petal on the Rose belonged to The Raven Queen of Fate who made a Pact with Vecna to twin their worlds to their mutual benefit.\n   116→\n   117→---\n   118→\n   119→### Slide 3\n   120→\n   121→The Death Pact\n   122→\n   123→The shared domain of Death would serve as the cornerstone of their agreement. This Pact is largely a mystery, however their specialties point to some grand revelation that will occur at the Fated time of the Raven Queen’s transition from Life to Death. This harvest of the limitless potential of the regent Goddess would culminate in a shared Secret with Vecna, God of Death and Secrets. The end result is a mutually beneficial understanding of What is Beyond the Death of Gods? Each believing they would best the other at the moment of the revelation as they were better equipped to take the advantage, the Pact was sealed.\n   124→\n   125→Mardov and Vodram, twinned worlds, were formed and with them 4 Zeniths capable of wielding the power of the domain of Death.\n   126→\n   127→---\n   128→\n   129→### Slide 4\n   130→\n   131→Zeniths\n   132→\n   133→From the Mountains to the Forest, in Winter or in Harvest, the Zeniths of power await their Anchor and Heart.\n   134→\n   135→There will be War and Death, Pestilence and Famine, but to Anchor the end of Guard’ Daemon, the battlefield will be tallied, its victor declared.\n   136→\n   137→For they wield a secret from beyond the grave of gods. Even in the fires of the Forge or the shadows of the Wilde, the light of Faith and the hope of Progenitors shall strike their Fated Heart.\n   138→\n   139→---\n   140→\n   141→### Slide 5\n   142→\n   143→Bringer of Oblivion\n   144→\n   145→Asmodeus, Bringer of Oblivion only took 100 years to find Mardov, and 12 months to signal the first Guard’Daemon. However, to truely seal Asmodeus away, Kolyma, the First and Last Curator of Mardov had to sacrifice himself to activate the Zeniths and turn them against Oblivion. Unable to fully destroy the God, Asmodeus was sealed in thirteen amulets, to be scattered to the guardians on the edges of existence.\n   146→\n   147→---\n   148→\n   149→### Slide 6\n   150→\n   151→Inheritors of the Mantle\n   152→\n   153→In the Death of Kolyma, the potential he captured to answer the Curator’s Plea was bestowed in four mantles.\n   154→\n   155→The Alabaster Mantle of Arcane Magic\n   156→\n   157→The Ivy Mantle of Natural Magic\n   158→\n   159→The Palladium Mantle of Divine Magic\n   160→\n   161→The Crystalline Mantle of Mortal Magic\n   162→"}]},"uuid":"322f727f-e4de-4bd8-b620-e2b880924f58","timestamp":"2026-03-19T05:58:48.465Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"33ef957a-f999-466a-ab0e-6087518a2544","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/eviemini/Documents/code/server-manager","sessionId":"e89c0432-c0b0-471a-97e7-f75f9aa647b4","version":"2.1.78","gitBranch":"main","slug":"stateful-seeking-melody"}

{"parentUuid":"2e75b3ad-f548-4844-98c4-1c1c2c21db75","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"616419db-f8ea-4cbf-8b1b-8e3d3d602562","agentId":"a7319d0ecd9725385","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01AGVaxbWdrE3u8q94e3Dxtq","type":"tool_result","content":"     1→---\n     2→type: lore\n     3→title: \"Story Arcs & Narrative Structure\"\n     4→---\n     5→\n     6→# Story Arcs & Narrative Structure\n     7→\n     8→## The Overarching Story\n     9→\n    10→The campaign revolves around [[Mordred|Ramses/Mordred]], whose plan unfolds in three stages:\n    11→\n    12→1. **Gets the Avatar of the [[Raven Queen]] to sacrifice her immortality** -- this creates a rift between [[Mardov]] and [[V-Mardov]] where [[Vecna]]'s Avatar rules\n    13→2. **Collects [[Sabine Ra|Sabine]]'s gifts to control her power** -- originally wanted to resurrect [[Sabine Ra|Sabine]], his lost love, but after her spirit shattered he pivoted to controlling her fragments. Gave up his phylactery to take out the [[Artificer's Guild]], gaining [[Xerxes]]'s body (corrupting the individual beings that make up [[Xerxes]])\n    14→3. **Destroys all the keystones** -- with his newfound body, [[Mordred]] has one goal: bring about the invasion of this realm by V-MORDRED by destroying all the keystones\n    15→\n    16→### The Keystones\n    17→\n    18→| Name | Gemstone | Location | Conflict Type | Horseman/Anchor |\n    19→|------|----------|----------|---------------|-----------------|\n    20→| [[Heart of the Wilde]] | Emerald | [[Forest's Zenith]] | Dungeon | Death |\n    21→| [[Heart of the Forge]] | Garnet | [[Mountain's Zenith]] | Dwarven underdark conflict | War |\n    22→| [[Heart of the Faith]] | Sapphire | [[Winter's Zenith]] | Winter's wasteland | Pestilence |\n    23→| [[Heart of the Progenitors]] | Ruby | [[Harvest's Zenith]] | Cataclysmic, philosopher's stone | Famine |\n    24→\n    25→---\n    26→\n    27→## Act I — The Awakening (Chapters 0-13)\n    28→\n    29→The party is recruited into the [[Scarlet Tunic Brigade]]. They encounter [[Mordred]] possessing [[Finkle Hollingsworth]], [[Sabine Ra]] is freed from her phylactery, and crystalline heart fragments are deposited in the party. Three prophecies are received from [[Ruth]], the Hags, and [[Mia]], establishing the four-zenith framework.\n    30→\n    31→**Key beats:**\n    32→- Party death and resurrection by [[Sabine Ra]] (Chapter 2)\n    33→- [[V-Mardov]] rift opens; [[Varis Liadon|Varris]] arrives from the alternate reality (Chapter 6)\n    34→- First, Second, and Third Prophecies received (Chapters 9, 12, 13)\n    35→- [[Mia]] reveals she wiped the party's memories\n    36→\n    37→---\n    38→\n    39→## Act II — The Voyage (Chapters 14-40)\n    40→\n    41→The party sails aboard the [[Thornberry Shade]], encounters the zodiac spirits (who bet their immortality on the champions), explores the [[Tomb of the Last Curator]], and splits into parallel tracks.\n    42→\n    43→**Key beats:**\n    44→- Tiefling prejudice arc; soul glamours for [[Nyx Khamestria|Nyx]] and [[Tesca Duskhallow|Tesca]] (Chapters 17-19)\n    45→- Zodiac spirits revealed; they bet their immortality on the champions (Chapter 23)\n    46→- Tomb of the Last Curator; four zenith hieroglyphics decoded (Chapters 24-25)\n    47→- [[Peraxis Phyrexia]] revealed; [[Reynaldine]]'s network exposed (Chapters 30-31)\n    48→- [[Thorkon Fireforge|Thorkon]]'s letter found inside [[Daina Fireforge|Daina]]'s axe; [[forgesworn]] lore revealed (Chapter 34)\n    49→\n    50→---\n    51→\n    52→## Act III — The Escalation (Chapters 41-86)\n    53→\n    54→Campaign splits into parallel tracks. Individual character arcs deepen.\n    55→\n    56→### The Cloud City Arc ([[windhaven]])\n    57→\n    58→The party arrives in [[windhaven]] via a portal from the [[Kraken of the Sand Seas]]. Key events:\n    59→\n    60→1. **[[Tiberius D. Rex]]** receives a premonition of the party's arrival\n    61→2. **Research at the Windmill Willow**: [[Brigit Wildfire|Brigitte]] researches the zodiacs (astral chart showing the 12 animals, one per month); [[Tesca Duskhallow|Tesca]] researches the [[Diadem of Dawn]]; [[Randal]] researches the gods\n    62→3. **[[Leonidas of Summer's Last Light]]** puts the party on trial in his sphinx demi-plane:\n    63→   - [[Varis Liadon|Varys]] requests help against [[Vecna]], is granted a vision of [[Daina Fireforge|Daina]] grabbing the [[Heart of the Forge]] and jumping into lava\n    64→   - [[Nyx Khamestria|Nyx]] requests help to save [[Xyn]], meets [[Juliannia of the Summer Court]] -- her real patron\n    65→   - [[Fen Darkeyes|Fen]] wishes he and his brother never fell under the Aboleth's influence; wish granted\n    66→   - [[Sprocket Overspark|Sprocket]] alone is deemed unworthy (meaning unknown)\n    67→   - [[Tiberius D. Rex|Tiberious]] has undergone this process 75 times, making him far older than the 76 he appears\n    68→4. **The Cabal of Horns**: [[Randal]] and [[Tesca Duskhallow|Tesca]] discover an underground tiefling society led by [[Winnipeg Redvine]], uncovering a child-kidnapping plot by Lady [[Mathia Nuum]]\n    69→5. **Reynaldine's Demand**: The party encounters [[Reynaldine|Reynaldine of the Autumn Court]] collecting Elemental Seals. [[Kelik Temptus]] dies when absorbed into his amulet. [[Reynaldine]] already has Fire and Earth seals; tasks the party with retrieving the Water amulet from [[Lapis Poseidon]]\n    70→6. **[[Brigit Wildfire|Brigitte]]'s Stag Form**: Discovers her stag form resembles [[Shi-Shi-Gami|Shi Shi Gami]], with antlers forming the [[Zodiac Locket]]\n    71→7. **Cloud City Falling**: The party fights to save Cloud City, encounters [[Zepheeron the Bronze]], battles at Jackfall Landing, and is rescued by [[Captain Liza]] on the [[Thornberry Wilde]]\n    72→8. **[[Reynaldine]]'s True Nature Revealed**: [[Randal]] uses Legend Lore and discovers [[Reynaldine]] is actually 4 beings: [[Juliannia of the Summer Court]], [[Graz'zt|Grazz't the Demon Prince]], [[Gabriel the Kind]] (Archangel), and [[Reynaldine|Reynardine, Prince of Harvest]]\n    73→9. **[[Nyx Khamestria|Nyx]] meets [[Xyn]]**: Via Lorelai's magical broach, the twin sisters finally communicate. [[Xyn]] reveals [[Gabriel the Kind]] is her patron (lives in teddy bear \"Mr. Happy\"), that the [[Mountain's Zenith]] opening released [[Barachiel the Righteous]], and that [[Euclid]]'s book *The Origin of Genasi and the Rebirth of the Progenitors* is the key to the [[Harvest's Zenith]]\n    74→10. **The Harvest's Zenith spotted**: [[Sprocket Overspark|Sprocket]] touches the satellite and sees a hanging upside-down city with the [[Harvest's Zenith]] -- a dual pyramid with 7 floors all rotating out of phase\n    75→\n    76→---\n    77→\n    78→## Act IV — The Endgame (Chapters 87-136)\n    79→\n    80→The threads converge. Character arcs resolve. Gods die. The world changes.\n    81→\n    82→**Key beats:**\n    83→- Second Reunion; TPK and revival via myconid network (Chapter 103)\n    84→- [[Reginald Providence]] killed; [[Anchor of War]] silenced; Therzadun thwarted (Chapters 104-105)\n    85→- Declaration of War on gods; [[Peraxis Phyrexia|Peraxis]] reveals complex motivations (Chapter 106)\n    86→- Ragnarok; Warforged awakening; [[Irethia]] revealed as aasimar (Chapter 107)\n    87→- [[lolth]]'s \"Two Truths and a Lie\" (Chapter 108)\n    88→- [[Asmodeus]] killed by [[Josh Coolbreeze]]; three specters escape, one enters [[Nocturnus Wayne]] (Chapter 136)\n    89→- [[Bergs Fireforge|Bergs]] becomes deity of [[Mardov]]'s [[Feywild]] via Stone Table sacrifice (Chapter 136)\n    90→\n    91→---\n    92→\n    93→## Global Conflicts\n    94→\n    95→### The Orc-Human War ([[DawnHaven]])\n    96→\n    97→**Location:** [[dawnpass]]/[[windhaven]]/[[Eavallan]] vs [[Stra'Franar]]/[[Fae'ish]]/[[Tygras Logath]]\n    98→\n    99→**Reveal:** At the [[Magister's Circle]], an envoy from [[dawnpass]] (notably containing zero Half-Orcs -- there have been none in the entire game) requests aid against an army of sorcerers wielding draconic-blood-fueled destruction similar to the Tiefling army that destroyed [[uma]].\n   100→\n   101→**Root cause:** The Orc Chieftain and former Gladiator Pit Champion ([[Logath HellEye|Logath]]) made a deal with the same fae demon as [[Tygras]], causing intense bloodlust and madness. This makes him a target for [[Mordred]], who uses Logath's influence to cut off food from the fertile Harvestlands north of [[windhaven]].\n   102→\n   103→**Connection to the Zenith:** This is the location of the [[Anchor of Famine]], tied to the [[Harvest's Zenith]]. The resurgence of Orc sorcerers matches exactly with when it last rained. The [[Matrim Pact]] was between human sailors of old [[uma]], gnomes of [[Fae'ish]], and [[Qual'Xar]] half-elves -- they promised sanctuary atop the [[Maturin Turtle]].\n   104→\n   105→**The Hidden City:** The city is actually a buried pyramid, the great ziggurat housing the [[Harvest's Zenith]]. Inside, [[Tygras]] himself (or the arcane being he became after his failed ascension) has bid his time feeding on internal zenith energies. Destroying his echo rips a hole in the Fae and reveals the fae demon who gave [[Nyx Khamestria|Nyx]] her powers.\n   106→\n   107→**Nyx's Choice:** The demon was the one who made the pact with [[Tygras]], and [[Nyx Khamestria|Nyx]] was his heir. By destroying the last echo of [[Tygras]], future tiefling children can be prevented. [[Nyx Khamestria|Nyx]] inherits the mark of [[Tygras]] (actually Sable's Beacon, an ancient fae marking). She can either rid the world of \"demon children\" or rebuild the lost empire by adding tieflings to the [[Matrim Pact]] and reviving the guardian turtle.\n   108→\n   109→**Reviving the Guardian:** Requires (1) removing the [[Anchor of Famine]] from the top of the zenith (guarded by the Harvest Effigy), (2) returning the [[Heart of the Progenitors]] to the crevasse in the middle of [[Tygras Logath]], (3) speaking the words: *\"On his back all vows are made, a haven for the continent of dawn paid.\"* Upon finishing, the turtle skeleton below the bay is revived -- a massive turtle carrying an Atlantean city, serving as THE landing place between [[Mardov]] and the [[Feywild]].\n   110→\n   111→---\n   112→\n   113→## Character Arc Themes\n   114→\n   115→### [[Sprocket Overspark]] — The Pursuit of Fun\n   116→\n   117→**Question:** Is it possible for responsibility to be fun?\n   118→\n   119→[[Sprocket Overspark|Sprocket]]'s fate as the next Avatar of the [[Raven Queen]] cannot be downplayed. No matter how he tries, this is his overarching conflict: *\"You cannot run from your fate.\"* To learn this is to understand what it means to become the next [[Raven Queen]].\n   120→\n   121→His rise of infamy: Trixie -> [[Thornberry Shade]] -> [[Qual'Xar]] -> [[Magister's Circle]] & [[Conquis Abbey]] -> Entire Material Plane. The deeds he deems \"fun\" provide notoriety, making it harder to be underestimated.\n   122→\n   123→### [[Brigit Wildfire]] — Self-Discovery\n   124→\n   125→**Question:** What makes me special?\n   126→\n   127→[[Brigit Wildfire|Brigitte]] is a [[Progenitors|Progenitor]] -- one of the original beings created in Eden. This grants her the ability to open the [[Harvest's Zenith]] without destroying the elemental seals. There is no reason for this other than fate -- the Progenitors were fated to return to help stop [[Gard'Daemon]].\n   128→\n   129→### [[Randal]] — Apotheosis and Pacifism\n   130→\n   131→**Question:** What does it take to become a god?\n   132→\n   133→[[Randal]]'s decision to become a pacifist is valiant but ultimately flawed, for he claimed victory while he still has goals outstanding. His ideal: *\"Victory is only satisfying if achieved without violence.\"*\n   134→\n   135→Foils to his pacifism:\n   136→- *\"Violence is Victory\"* -- [[Logath HellEye|Logath]], Orcish Leader\n   137→- *\"Peace is what I will achieve after all my enemies have been defeated by my hand\"* -- [[Evelyandrin of the Spring Court]]\n   138→- *\"Some things are worth fighting for\"* -- [[Reynaldine]]\n   139→- *\"Nothing is worth fighting for, I will endure and adapt\"* -- [[Grumblesin of the Winter Court]]\n   140→\n   141→---\n   142→\n   143→## Player Background Tie-Ins\n   144→\n   145→### [[Sprocket Overspark]]\n   146→- Has a yurt outside [[hinderstap]] village in [[monethern]]\n   147→- Sent by [[lillith]], the Platinum Seat of the Diviners Covenant, to investigate a village where inhabitants are normal by day but ravenous and murderous by night\n   148→- Being killed in the village forces the murdered to stay permanently, reborn each morning (happened to Giddean Clary, Mustard Bard, with his Basset Hound Darling)\n   149→- A portal/wound links [[hinderstap]] to the [[shadowfell]]\n   150→\n   151→### [[Brigit Wildfire]]\n   152→- Criminal contact: [[Firekeeper Morn Loregramar|Firekeeper Lorgrammar]], orc shaman whose people betrayed him and assaulted [[Eavallan]] Forest, [[Stra'Franar]], and Last Tower Bridge\n   153→- She is a [[Progenitors|Progenitor]], one of the original beings created in Eden\n   154→- [[Eavallan]] Forest is of the [[Feywild]], the earliest location where elves first came to [[Mardov]]\n   155→- The original sacking of [[Eavallan]] by the orcs set into motion the steps to open the [[Harvest's Zenith]]\n   156→- Progenitor blood was thought to be in [[uma]], but the bloodline was still strong; [[Brigit Wildfire|Brigitte]]'s parents were drawn to [[Eavallan]] because it was closest to the Zenith\n   157→\n   158→---\n   159→\n   160→## Story Beats Still To Hit\n   161→\n   162→### [[Randal]] and Apotheosis\n   163→- The gods were the first to uncover universal truths\n   164→- Once a truth is uncovered, Apotheosis becomes possible (see [[lore/cosmology|Cosmology]])\n   165→- [[Reynaldine]]'s plan to collapse all planes is his miracle attempt\n   166→\n   167→### The Raven Queen's Transition\n   168→- The Death Pact between the [[Raven Queen]] and [[Vecna]] hinges on the Raven Queen's transition from Life to Death\n   169→- The harvest of the regent Goddess's limitless potential will culminate in a shared Secret with [[Vecna]]\n   170→- Both believe they will best the other at the moment of revelation\n   171→"}]},"uuid":"0b16e7cc-6491-4a44-8683-d107af8db519","timestamp":"2026-03-19T05:58:48.925Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"2e75b3ad-f548-4844-98c4-1c1c2c21db75","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/eviemini/Documents/code/server-manager","sessionId":"e89c0432-c0b0-471a-97e7-f75f9aa647b4","version":"2.1.78","gitBranch":"main","slug":"stateful-seeking-melody"}

Each character's journey is tracked chronologically through party splits, reunions, and pivotal moments.\n    10→\n    11→> **Confidence Legend**: [HIGH] = directly from player notes or handouts. [MEDIUM] = inferred from structural evidence. [LOW] = reconstructed from context.\n    12→\n    13→---\n    14→\n    15→## BRIGIT (Wild Spirit of Fire)\n    16→\n    17→**Player**: Liz\n    18→**Class**: Druid (Circle of the Moon) / Barbarian multiclass\n    19→**Race**: Changeling/Shapeshifter\n    20→**Key Items**: Anchor of Death (locket), Stag Zodiac\n    21→**Track**: Tieflings -> Distillery -> Good Guys Guild\n    22→\n    23→### Arc Summary\n    24→\n    25→Brigit begins as a chaotic, multi-formed druid struggling with the many voices in her head. She becomes a Progenitor through a ritual in Eden with the Children of the Forest, gains custody of the Anchor of Death in her locket, and carries the Stag Zodiac. Her arc is one of identity -- learning to channel her rage and multiplicity into purpose. She is the Wild Spirit of Fire, and through her shapeshifting, serves as the party's most versatile combatant and emotional anchor. By Act 4 she has ascended the locket, lost her elemental powers during the Harvest Zenith battle, and must find her way forward without her primary coping mechanism.\n    26→\n    27→### Timeline\n    28→\n    29→| Chapter | Event | Significance |\n    30→|---------|-------|-------------|\n    31→| 0-2 | Joins the party; party killed and resurrected by Sabine Ra | [HIGH] Establishes the campaign's death/rebirth theme |\n    32→| 5-7 | Hall of the Raven; early adventures | [MEDIUM] Party cohesion building |\n...\n</persisted-output>"}]},"uuid":"62faff05-ba12-4668-9166-3c14ca2a30af","timestamp":"2026-03-19T05:58:49.425Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"e7cc26af-c76e-4fa6-b3e3-7e4afd67ac41","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/eviemini/Documents/code/server-manager","sessionId":"e89c0432-c0b0-471a-97e7-f75f9aa647b4","version":"2.1.78","gitBranch":"main","slug":"stateful-seeking-melody"}

{"parentUuid":"c258243f-eaa4-4691-8eda-77fee912625b","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"616419db-f8ea-4cbf-8b1b-8e3d3d602562","agentId":"a7319d0ecd9725385","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_011ax6p1nYoHVNAjritGHUXn","type":"tool_result","content":"     1→---\n     2→type: character\n     3→role: npc-major\n     4→name: \"Vecna Sworn\"\n     5→aliases: [\"Agents of Vecna\"]\n     6→race: \"Various\"\n     7→class: \"Various\"\n     8→affiliation: \"[[Vecna]]\"\n     9→location: \"Across Mardov and beyond\"\n    10→status: various\n    11→first_appearance: \"Chapter 0\"\n    12→source: [\"NPCs/Vecna Sworn/\", \"NPC Catalog\"]\n    13→art: []\n    14→---\n    15→\n    16→## Overview\n    17→\n    18→The Vecna Sworn are the campaign's primary antagonist faction -- agents of [[Vecna]], the God of Secrets, working to assault the four Zeniths and bring about Vecna's ascension. They are organized by Zenith target.\n    19→\n    20→## Bynar & Pestilence Zenith\n    21→\n    22→| Name | Role |\n    23→|------|------|\n    24→| Garth | Pestilence Keeper |\n    25→| Essy Lin | Ex White Dragon Guard of the Winter's Zenith |\n    26→| Umangi Shiva | Highest Priestess of the Yuan-Ti |\n    27→\n    28→## Machu & Death Zenith\n    29→\n    30→| Name | Role |\n    31→|------|------|\n    32→| **[[Mordred]]** | Primary Vecna agent. Disguised as [[Finkle]] in the pilot. Multiple forms. |\n    33→| Salina | Mordred's Death Knight Captain |\n    34→| Costa Una | Traitor of the [[Scarlet Tunic Brigade]] |\n    35→| Lich [[Ned'Zailok]] | Keeper of Death's Anchor. Used by Vecna for experiments. The lich who destroyed [[Tower Bridge]] -- [[Varis Laindon]]'s nemesis. |\n    36→| Lord Soth | Death Knight of [[vodram]] |\n    37→| Reva | Machu Royal Advisor, cause of corruption |\n    38→| Tatiana | The Imposter of Machu |\n    39→| Nolando Esquir | Underworld Kingpin |\n    40→| Corrupt Xerxes | End boss of Forest Zenith |\n    41→\n    42→## Tygras Logar & Famine Zenith\n    43→\n    44→| Name | Role |\n    45→|------|------|\n    46→| **[[Reynaldine]]** (True Form) | Fey Lord serving Vecna. NOT to be confused with [[Reynardine]] (Nyx's patron). |\n    47→| Logath HellEye | Emperor of Tygras Logath. Descendant of gladiator orcs. |\n    48→| Gordan BlackHand | Orc Sorcerer and Agent of Vecna |\n    49→| Grim'Nak the Terrible | |\n    50→| Jafar | Multiple depictions |\n    51→| Sledge | Orc Chieftain |\n    52→\n    53→## Underdark & War Zenith\n    54→\n    55→| Name | Role |\n    56→|------|------|\n    57→| **[[Oblivion Wayne]]** | [[Nocturnus Wayne]]'s aunt. Leader of the Drow. Alpha vampire. Obsessed with the ancient Warforged army. |\n    58→| **[[Ebon Wayne]]** | New Patriarch of the Waynes. Vampire drow. Captor of the [[Bat Zodiac]]. Uses it to create alpha vampires. |\n    59→| **[[Peraxis Geth]]** (Peraxis Phyrexia) | Illithilich raised by Vecna's power. Gifted a shard of the Raven Queen. Creator of the Spell Plague. Betrayed [[Thorkon Fireforge]]. |\n    61→| Grumpkin Tassle | Minister of War |\n    62→| Alhoon Pollivar | Illithid researcher. Discovered vampirism liberates mindflayers from Elder Brain. |\n    64→\n    65→## Key Lore\n    66→\n    67→- The **Elder Brain** has been corrupted by Vecna's influence through Peraxis. Only Ulitharid elite escaped.\n    68→- Oblivion uses **vampire alpha power** to depose her brother and unite the Drow. {"parentUuid":"2cc6fbee-3cd7-476c-8555-ff2dc903072c","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"616419db-f8ea-4cbf-8b1b-8e3d3d602562","agentId":"a7319d0ecd9725385","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_0186eT5CfHMcScmCdfqGnk2r","type":"tool_result","content":"     1→---\n     2→type: faction\n     3→name: \"Vecna Sworn\"\n     4→aliases: [\"Vecna's Four Zeniths\", \"The Zenith Operations\"]\n     5→category: planar\n     6→leader: \"[[Vecna]]\"\n     7→headquarters: \"[[V-Mardov]]\"\n     8→status: active\n     9→source: [\"intrigue-web.md\", \"factions-and-orgs.md\"]\n    10→---\n    11→\n    12→# Vecna Sworn\n    13→\n    14→## Overview\n    15→\n    16→[[Vecna]]'s conquest of [[Mardov]] is organized through four domains of power — the **Four Zeniths**. Each Zenith has a lieutenant, a target, and a nested web of agents. [[Vecna]] rules [[V-Mardov]] (a parallel dimension mirroring the material plane) and commands all four operations.\n    17→\n    18→**Ultimate Goal:** Destroy all keystones, enable [[V-Mardov|V-MORDRED]]'s invasion of the material plane.\n    19→\n    20→## The Four Zenith Operations\n    21→\n    22→### Zenith of Death (Forest's Zenith — Machu)\n    23→- **Heart:** [[Heart of the Wilde]] (Emerald)\n    24→- **Anchor:** [[Anchor of Death]] (obsidian sword)\n    25→- **Eye:** Green Eye of [[Vecna]]\n    26→- **Primary Agent:** [[Mordred]] / [[Mordred|Ramses]]\n    27→  - [[Scarlet Tunic Brigade]] (soul-harvesting front)\n    28→  - [[Jorgen]] (lure, turned revenant)\n    29→  - [[Golden Reach]] demons ([[mandu|Markus]]/Danelle, incubus/succubus in Argos)\n    30→  - Hag Coven (Margaret, Gertrude, Tabitha — brew Pestilence from children's souls)\n    31→  - [[mandu]] (corrupt Argos guard captain)\n    32→  - Reva (Machu Royal Advisor)\n    33→  - Tatiana (the Imposter of Machu)\n    34→  - [[jafar]] (Sally's uncle, hired the Dons, placed [[Nathan Yanagan]] as spy)\n    35→  - [[Costa Una]] (traitor within STB)\n    36→  - Salina (Death Knight Captain), Lord Soth (Death Knight of Vodram)\n    37→- **Secondary Agent:** [[Ned'Zailok]] (Lich, Keeper of Death's Anchor in [[V-Mardov]])\n    38→  - Betrayed Casaval Dekun (Eladrin warrior)\n    39→- **Boss:** Forest Guardian / Forest Titan — awakened by [[Mordred]] using the [[Heart of the Wilde]]. Defeating it transports party to [[V-Mardov]].\n    40→\n    41→### Zenith of War (Mountain's Zenith — Vag'Mahar / Underdark)\n    42→- **Heart:** [[Heart of the Forge]] (Garnet)\n    43→- **Anchor:** [[Anchor of War]] (hammer-axe-javelin)\n    44→- **Eye:** Red Eye of [[Vecna]]\n    45→- **Primary Agent:** [[Peraxis Phyrexia]] (Illithilich — THE Central Villain)\n    46→  - Created the [[Spell Plague]] (with [[Alhoon Ren]]'s research)\n    47→  - Captured the [[Zodiac|Stag Zodiac]] to locate [[Mountain's Zenith]]\n    48→  - Holds [[Thorkon Fireforge]] captive; plans Ceremorphosis\n    49→  - Beholder relay network for psychic communication\n    50→  - Homunculus Thraalm (killed Ch. 66)\n    51→  - **Research Arm:** [[Alhoon Ren]] (double agent, secretly taking notes) + [[Alhoon Pollivar]] (Umber Hulk facility)\n    52→- **Allied Agent:** [[Oblivion Wayne]] (Vampire Queen of the Drow)\n    53→  - [[Ebon Wayne]] (husband, discovered [[Spell Plague]], captured Bat Zodiac)\n    54→  - Vampire-converted Drow army\n    55→  - Plans overland invasion\n    56→- **Allied Agent:** [[Ghost-Eye Thorrin]] (Puppetmaster of [[Vag'Mahar]])\n    57→  - Convinced [[Thorkon Fireforge|Thorkon]] to betray [[Thorson Bronzebeard II]]\n    58→  - Then betrayed the deal; hunted all [[forgesworn]]\n    59→  - Missing by late campaign\n    60→- **Nested Conspiracy:** The Dwarven Civil War\n    61→  - Three Thanes: Blackbeard, Brownbeard, Redbeard\n    62→  - Three secret police: Blackguard, Truth's Light, Silent Flame\n    63→  - **ALL THREE secretly run by A SINGLE MIND FLAYER**\n    64→  - Goal: use [[Anchor of War]] to breed better Duergar\n    65→\n    66→### Zenith of Pestilence (Winter's Zenith — Bynar)\n    67→- **Heart:** [[Heart of the Faith]] (Sapphire)\n    68→- **Anchor:** [[Anchor of Pestilence]] (pan flute / Horn of Winter)\n    69→- **Eye:** Blue Eye of [[Vecna]]\n    70→- **Primary Agent:** [[rimefang]] (Undead Dragon)\n    71→  - Raised by [[Vecna]], given a white dragon body via [[Black Hand Clan]] rebirth ritual\n    72→  - Ordered to \"open the [[Winter's Zenith]] and finish the Spellplague\"\n    73→  - Receives pestilence supply shipments\n    74→  - Made one last ride as sacrifice (Ch. 113)\n    75→- **Allied Agent:** Yuan-Ti / [[Scales of Falak]]\n    76→  - Stole the [[Anchor of Pestilence]] from the [[Sunless Citadel]]\n    77→  - Ruined the [[Winter's Curators]] (dragon-kin tribe)\n    78→  - Leaders: Umangi Shiva, Casstantinople Pserephonie, Lady Sessura Vaught, Mathia Nuum, Bree Jasnar\n    79→- **Guardian:** [[Frostragan]] (White Dragon, stole [[Heart of the Faith]])\n    80→- **Tiamat Connection:** [[Oblivion Wayne]] gave her soul to [[Tiamat]]. [[Tiamat]]'s avatar awakened late campaign.\n    81→\n    82→### Zenith of Famine (Harvest's Zenith — Tygras Logath / DawnHaven)\n    83→- **Heart:** [[Heart of the Progenitors]] (Ruby)\n    84→- **Anchor:** [[Anchor of Famine]] ([[Diadem of Dawn]])\n    85→- **Eye:** Orange Eye of [[Vecna]]\n    86→- **Primary Agent:** [[Reynaldine]] (Fey Lord of the Autumn Court) — **TRIPLE AGENT**\n    87→  - Serves [[Vecna]] but has his OWN agenda\n    88→  - Wants [[DawnHaven]] cleansed of [[uma]]'s remnants to find the [[Diadem of Dawn|Diadem]]\n    89→  - Hired Annie Hallowbrook to bring [[Kelik Waystar]] to [[windhaven]]\n    90→  - Used [[Xyn]] as messenger\n    91→  - **True Plan (Ch. 133):** Orchestrated bringing ALL villains together at the Convergence + Stone Table so he could ascend to godhood and save Gabriel\n    92→  - Invoked the Ritual of the Stone Table through Umbara Skuttle\n    93→- **Allied Agent:** [[Logath HellEye]] (Emperor of [[Tygras Logath]])\n    94→  - Former gladiator pit champion\n    95→  - Made deal with same Fae demon as [[Tygras]]\n    96→  - Causes bloodlust and madness among orcs\n    97→- **The Orc-Human War** — manufactured by [[Reynaldine]]/[[Logath HellEye|Logath]]/[[Mordred]]\n    98→  - [[Gordan BlackHand]] (Orc Sorcerer, Vecna agent)\n    99→  - [[Grimnak Norman|Grim'Nak the Terrible]], Sledge (Orc Chieftains)\n   100→- **Sealed Evil:** [[Tygras]] — Ancient tiefling warlord sealed inside the Zenith, feeding off its energy\n   101→\n   102→## Allies & Enemies\n   103→\n   104→### Allies\n   105→- [[Therzadun Sworn]] (entangled but separate)\n   106→- [[Graz'zt]] (Demon Prince, held [[Heart of the Progenitors]])\n   107→- [[Asmodeus]] (sealed with 10/13 seals — killed Ch. 136)\n   108→\n   109→### Enemies\n   110→- The Awakened (the party)\n   111→- [[Artificer's Guild]]\n   112→- [[Conquis Abbey]]\n   113→- The Zodiac spirits\n   114→- [[Silver Scales of Bahamut]]\n   115→"}]},"uuid":"1ac7ef5a-4723-4325-84f0-0a9a829f8882","timestamp":"2026-03-19T05:59:03.284Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"2cc6fbee-3cd7-476c-8555-ff2dc903072c","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/eviemini/Documents/code/server-manager","sessionId":"e89c0432-c0b0-471a-97e7-f75f9aa647b4","version":"2.1.78","gitBranch":"main","slug":"stateful-seeking-melody"}

{"parentUuid":"a1bdc356-d544-4826-bcd2-3e8e0c3f1735","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"616419db-f8ea-4cbf-8b1b-8e3d3d602562","agentId":"a7319d0ecd9725385","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01VQfQxKkPVaYgMw3nyus1CQ","type":"tool_result","content":"     1→---\n     2→type: location\n     3→category: city\n     4→name: Machu\n     5→parent: \"[[DawnHaven]]\"\n     6→aliases:\n     7→  - The Dark Port\n     8→notable_npcs:\n     9→  - \"[[Jafar Lepidoptera]]\"\n    10→  - \"[[Hope Lepidoptera]]\"\n    11→  - \"[[Mordred]]\"\n    12→  - \"[[ramses]]\"\n    13→  - \"[[Xerxes]]\"\n    14→  - \"[[Ned'Zailok]]\"\n    15→chapters:\n    16→  - Act 3\n    17→  - Act 4\n    18→source: Places/DawnHaven/Machu/\n    19→maps: []\n    20→tags:\n    21→  - city\n    22→  - machu\n    23→  - dawnhaven\n    24→  - forest-zenith\n    25→  - death\n    26→---\n    27→\n    28→# Machu\n    29→\n    30→A grand port city in [[DawnHaven]], Machu is a place of faded grandeur and creeping darkness. Its Venetian-inspired architecture -- domed cathedrals, angel-topped columns, ornate bridges over canals -- is rendered in permanent sunset, as if the city exists in an eternal twilight. Machu houses the [[Forest's Zenith]], containing the Heart of the Wilde (emerald) and the Anchor of Death (obsidian sword). The city is ruled by the mad King [[Jafar Lepidoptera]], trapped in madness by [[Mordred]]/Ramses.\n    31→\n    32→## Geography & Architecture\n    33→\n    34→### Landscape\n    35→The reference art shows Machu as a massive harbor city at sunset. Tall-masted ships crowd the docks in the foreground. Behind them, the city rises in layers -- canal-side warehouses, ornate bridges with winged angel statuary, domed cathedrals and spired towers climbing toward the skyline. Smoke and haze hang over the industrial districts. Beyond the city, mountain peaks and the Forest's Zenith temple complex are visible.\n    36→\n    37→### Architectural Style\n    38→Renaissance-Venetian with dark fantasy undertones. Stone bridges with golden angel statues, copper-green domed cathedrals, columned government buildings, and dense urban blocks with terracotta and slate roofing. The Palace features a royal temple and throne room. The Circle of the Murder (Castle Von Vicktonstein) has a distinctly gothic horror feel -- cobblestone streets, raven altars, zombie-filled laboratories. The Forest's Zenith temple is ancient and monumental, with Mesoamerican ziggurat influences.\n    39→\n    40→### Color Palette\n    41→Dominated by burning orange and amber -- the entire city appears bathed in a copper-gold sunset that never ends. Dark silhouettes of ships and buildings against the fiery sky. Deep shadows in the alleys. The Palace interiors are gilded. The Forest's Zenith shifts to deep green, moonlight silver, and ancient stone grey.\n    42→\n    43→### Lighting & Atmosphere\n    44→Perpetual dusk. The reference art is drenched in warm, smoky amber light -- industrial haze and sunset combining to create an atmosphere of decadent decline. This is a city past its prime, beautiful but rotting from within. The Forest's Zenith areas shift to cooler, more mystical lighting -- moonlit glades and spectral green.\n    45→\n    46→### Scale & Density\n    47→Large and dense. A major port with extensive harbor facilities, multiple districts, and surrounding outskirts along the Mortis River.\n    48→\n    49→## Districts\n    50→\n    51→### City Proper\n    52→The main urban area with docks, palace, and the ziggurat.\n    53→\n    54→### The Palace\n    55→Seat of the Lepidoptera dynasty.\n    56→- **Royalty:** [[Jafar Lepidoptera]] (mad king), [[Hope Lepidoptera]] (son of Jafar)\n    57→- **Old Guard:** [[Hysteria Lepidoptera]] (former queen), Korth Deikun, Twallia Deikun\n    58→- Connected to [[Ned'Zailok|Lich Ned'zailok]], Keeper of Death's Anchor\n    59→\n    60→### Circle of the Murder\n    61→Gothic horror district -- Castle Von Vicktonstein and its coven.\n    62→- Raven Altar, ghost town streets, coven interiors\n    63→- Laboratories (multiple): alchemical labs, Stomble's lab\n    64→- Zombie Artificers workshop\n    65→\n    66→### City Outskirts\n    67→The approaches to Machu along the Mortis River.\n    68→- **Above The Mortis River:** Machu Toad Pools, Mortis River Canyon, mountain peaks, Pride Rock\n    69→- Shipwreck Valley\n    70→- Machu entrance gates\n    71→\n    72→### Forest's Zenith\n    73→The ancient temple complex within Machu.\n    74→- Grand entrance with sky-reaching architecture\n    75→- Temple interiors (day and night versions)\n    76→- [[ramses]] -- Preserved Mummy within the Zenith\n    77→- [[Xerxes]] Reborn -- the soul at the bottom of the Zenith\n    78→\n    79→### Docks\n    80→Harbor district with ships and canal access. Entry point from [[Qual'Xar]] sea routes.\n    81→\n    82→## Key NPCs\n    83→\n    84→- **[[Jafar Lepidoptera]]** -- King of Machu, trapped in madness\n    85→- **[[Hope Lepidoptera]]** -- Son of Jafar\n    86→- **[[Mordred]]** / [[ramses]] -- The madness prisoner; Ramses's preserved mummy is in the Forest's Zenith\n    87→- **[[Ned'Zailok]]** -- Lich, Keeper of Death's Anchor, connected to [[varris]]\n    88→- **[[Mistrinel Tonrquai]]** -- Machu Freedom Fighter Leader\n    89→- **[[Messanana Gild]]** -- Priestess of Gold\n    90→- **[[Sannadari Vycount]]** -- Priestess of Pain\n    91→- **[[Zail Edok]]** -- Wild Necromancer\n    92→\n    93→## History\n    94→\n    95→Machu's crisis is the **Forest's Madness**. King [[Jafar Lepidoptera]] is trapped in madness -- [[Mordred]]/Ramses and his phylactery are the source. The Forest's Zenith within the city contains the Heart of the Wilde (emerald) and the Anchor of Death (obsidian sword, originally black steel, now appearing as black glass with a lingering curse).\n    96→\n    97→Resolution requires: using a sun-reflecting mirror to access the Zenith, freeing the king from madness, and reconciling the \"star-crossed three\" -- [[Xerxes]] (soul at the Zenith's bottom), [[ramses]] (soul in Daina's bag, needs to return to phylactery for cleansing), and [[Sabine Ra]] (who appears as a flash when both bodies merge into a demi-lich). After defeating the Forest Zenith boss, the party will be transported to [[V-MARDOV]].\n    98→\n    99→The six-fingered pet Balor in Jafar's throne room hints at the demonic corruption underlying the madness.\n   100→\n   101→## Gallery\n   102→\n   103→![[assets/maps-source/DawnHaven/Machu/MACHU.jpg]]\n   104→![[assets/maps-source/DawnHaven/Machu/machu entrance.jpg]]\n   105→![[assets/maps-source/DawnHaven/Machu/Machu Proper -- a view from the foot of the Ziggurat.jpg]]\n   106→![[assets/maps-source/DawnHaven/Machu/Machu royal temple.jpg]]\n   107→![[assets/maps-source/DawnHaven/Machu/Docks.jpg]]\n   108→![[assets/maps-source/DawnHaven/Machu/Forest's Zenith/forrest zenith grand entrance.jpg]]\n   109→![[assets/maps-source/DawnHaven/Machu/Forest's Zenith/forrest zenith temple day.jpg]]\n   110→![[assets/maps-source/DawnHaven/Machu/Forest's Zenith/forrest zenith temple night.jpg]]\n   111→![[assets/maps-source/DawnHaven/Machu/Circle of the Murder/Circle of the Murder.jpg]]\n   112→![[assets/maps-source/DawnHaven/Machu/Circle of the Murder/Castle Von Vicktonstein.jpg]]\n   113→"}]},"uuid":"1778e9b2-87de-45c7-a75f-b373f0fc602b","timestamp":"2026-03-19T05:59:04.364Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"a1bdc356-d544-4826-bcd2-3e8e0c3f1735","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/eviemini/Documents/code/server-manager","sessionId":"e89c0432-c0b0-471a-97e7-f75f9aa647b4","version":"2.1.78","gitBranch":"main","slug":"stateful-seeking-melody"}

{"parentUuid":"e2b9d830-1806-4460-bb70-bfe902d0fbb4","isSidechain":true,"agentId":"a7319d0ecd9725385","type":"progress","data":{"type":"hook_progress","hookEvent":"PostToolUse","hookName":"PostToolUse:Read","command":"\"$CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR/.claude/hooks/check-discord-queue.sh\""},"parentToolUseID":"toolu_01HHV9oXsBekfXwu2nRhgBX5","toolUseID":"toolu_01HHV9oXsBekfXwu2nRhgBX5","timestamp":"2026-03-19T05:59:04.530Z","uuid":"62007bf0-09da-48ba-9f84-7ce96fc706fc","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/eviemini/Documents/code/server-manager","sessionId":"e89c0432-c0b0-471a-97e7-f75f9aa647b4","version":"2.1.78","gitBranch":"main","slug":"stateful-seeking-melody"}
{"parentUuid":"62007bf0-09da-48ba-9f84-7ce96fc706fc","isSidechain":true,"agentId":"a7319d0ecd9725385","type":"progress","data":{"type":"hook_progress","hookEvent":"PostToolUse","hookName":"PostToolUse:Read","command":"callback"},"parentToolUseID":"toolu_01HHV9oXsBekfXwu2nRhgBX5","toolUseID":"toolu_01HHV9oXsBekfXwu2nRhgBX5","timestamp":"2026-03-19T05:59:04.530Z","uuid":"b692de6c-e29e-47b2-93fb-86651649dc63","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/eviemini/Documents/code/server-manager","sessionId":"e89c0432-c0b0-471a-97e7-f75f9aa647b4","version":"2.1.78","gitBranch":"main","slug":"stateful-seeking-melody"}
{"parentUuid":"e2b9d830-1806-4460-bb70-bfe902d0fbb4","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"616419db-f8ea-4cbf-8b1b-8e3d3d602562","agentId":"a7319d0ecd9725385","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01HHV9oXsBekfXwu2nRhgBX5","type":"tool_result","content":"     1→---\n     2→type: lore-extraction\n     3→title: \"story-arc-dm-notes\"\n     4→source: [\"NFS/lore-extraction/story-arc-dm-notes.md\"]\n     5→---\n     6→\n     7→# Mardov Story Arc — DM's Meta-Narrative and Plans\n     8→\n     9→*Extracted from DM's personal notes: story arch ideas.txt, dnd overarching story.txt, conflicts.txt, Prophesies and Zeniths.docx, Zodiacs and Player Symbolism.txt, ideas.txt, story idea bites.txt*\n    10→\n    11→---\n    12→\n    13→## The Core Thesis\n    14→\n    15→The entire campaign is built on the concept of **twin souls** — a cosmic mechanic originating from the gods Vecna and Sabine, who were the original twin souls. Vecna's jealousy of Sabine's additional powers (she rules life AND death, making her also the goddess of fate) drives the entire meta-narrative. Every major conflict is a fractal echo of this divine sibling rivalry.\n    16→\n    17→---\n    18→\n    19→## Mordred's Arc (The Primary Antagonist)\n    20→\n    21→### Phase 1: The Lover\n    22→Mordred originally wanted to resurrect Sabine, his lost love. This is the sympathetic core of his villainy.\n    23→\n    24→### Phase 2: The Collector\n    25→After Sabine's spirit was shattered, he pivoted to collecting her gifts and controlling her power. This mirrors what V-MORDRED does in the parallel dimension.\n    26→\n    27→### Phase 3: The Conspirator\n    28→- Convinced the Avatar of the Raven Queen to sacrifice her immortality\n    29→- This sacrifice created a rift between the material world and V-MARDOV (Vecna's domain)\n    30→- Gave up his own phylactery to infiltrate and destroy the Artificer's Guild\n    31→- This granted him Xerxes's body (corrupting the beings that compose Xerxes)\n    32→\n    33→### Phase 4: The Herald\n    34→With his new body, Mordred has a single goal: destroy all keystones to enable V-MORDRED's invasion. He is no longer acting for love but as an agent of the apocalypse.\n    35→\n    36→---\n    37→\n    38→## The Four-Zenith Structure\n    39→\n    40→The DM has designed the campaign as four parallel quest lines, each involving:\n    41→1. A **geographic conflict** (war, famine, pestilence, or systemic corruption)\n    42→2. A **Zenith** to cleanse (ancient nexus of power)\n    43→3. An **Anchor** to recover (weapon tied to a Horseman)\n    44→4. A **Heart** to secure (gemstone keystone)\n    45→5. **Three player characters** whose personal arcs interweave with the conflict\n    46→6. A **final battle station** where one player mans each Zenith\n    47→\n    48→### Arc 1: Harvest's Zenith (Famine) — Tygras Logath / DawnHaven\n    49→\n    50→**The Conflict:** Orc-Human war. Drought has destroyed the Harvestlands. An orc chieftain, corrupted by the same fae demon that empowered Tygras, is besieging DawnPass.\n    51→\n    52→**The Discovery:** The resurgence of orc sorcerers matches to the day with the last time it rained — supernatural famine. The Matrim Pact (humans, gnomes, half-elves united on the Maturin Turtle) holds the key.\n    53→\n    54→**The Dungeon:** The Harvest's Zenith is inside a buried pyramid (the great ziggurat), where Tygras himself still exists as an arcane being, feeding off the Zenith's internal energies.\n    55→\n    56→**The Choice:** After destroying Tygras's echo, Nyx meets the fae demon. She inherits the Mark of Tygras (Sable's Beacon). She can either rid the world of \"demon children\" (end tieflings) or rebuild the lost empire by adding tieflings to the Matrim Pact.\n    57→\n    58→**The Revival:** To revive the Maturin guardian:\n    59→1. Remove the Anchor of Famine from the zenith top (guarded by the Harvest Effigy)\n    60→2. Return the Heart of the Progenitors to the center of Tygras Logath\n    61→3. Speak: \"On his back all vows are made, a haven for the continent of dawn paid.\"\n    62→4. The turtle skeleton beneath the bay revives — a massive turtle carrying an Atlantean city, serving as THE landing point between the material world and the Fae\n    63→\n    64→**Player Arcs:**\n    65→- **Nyx (Owl):** Discovers her patron's last patronee was Tygras. Her sister Xyn's soul was the price. Wields Heart of Progenitors for all warlock rituals.\n    66→- **Irethia (Fox):** Discovers Aasimar vs. Tiefling distinction. Mans the Harvest Zenith in final battle.\n    67→- **Tesca (Mouse):** Discovers the Diadem of Dawn IS the Anchor of Famine — one wish that permanently removes spellcasting. Needed to destroy Tygras's Fae-Machine.\n    68→\n    69→### Arc 2: Winter's Zenith (Pestilence) — Bynar\n    70→\n    71→**The Conflict:** The dragon-kin tribe (Winter's Curators) is ruined. A fae eel serpent has swallowed the Heart of the Faith. Yuan-Ti stole the Anchor of Pestilence.\n    72→\n    73→**The Discovery:** The Sunless Citadel in the Shifting Sands is a Yuan-Ti fortress and shrine to the Serpent's Chosen. There is no Winter's Zenith at the citadel — the real one is within the mountain of Bynar, guarded by the Winter Dragon (White Dragon).\n    74→\n    75→**The Irony:** Stalwart's drive for revenge leads him to the truth. There's no zenith where he expected one, but he finds the Shield of Retribution instead.\n    76→\n    77→**Player Arcs:**\n    78→- **Randal (Turtle):** Must find the pan flute and resurrect the turtle Mia. The pan flute IS the Anchor of Pestilence. Grants anti-inspiration (debuff enemies) and group inspiration.\n    79→- **Stalwart (Mongoose):** Discovers Heart of Faith was stolen by White Dragon. Shield of Retribution + Heart of Faith = channel divine magic without a deity (pure retribution).\n    80→- **Dallor (Serpent):** Determines the Sunless Citadel is a Serpent's Chosen shrine. The ascended will call the serpent to defend her. Mans Winter's Zenith in final battle.\n    81→\n    82→### Arc 3: Mountain's Zenith (War) — Vag Mahar\n    83→\n    84→**The Conflict:** Dwarven civil war. Three Thanes squabble. Three secret police forces (Blackguard, Truth's Light, Silent Flame) are all secretly controlled by a single Mind Flayer.\n    85→\n    86→**The Discovery:** The Mind Flayer wants the Anchor of War (a hammer capable of earthquake-sinking Vag Mahar) to breed better Duergar. The forgotten First Forge must be relit.\n    87→\n    88→**The Resolution:** The bastard Dorrin (connected to Bergs/Daina) is the only one who can build/control Warforged. His golem Roc proves his lineage. He must be crowned Paragon.\n    89→\n    90→**Player Arcs:**\n    91→- **Daina (Wolverine):** Forgelord's daughter, twin soul with Bergs. Wields Anchor of War (half hammer-half axe, half javelin) to harness the First Flame and transform Bergs.\n    92→- **Bergs (Wolf):** Must give up his wild/natural self. Heir to First Forge. Becomes a Warforged powered by Heart of the Forge. When combined with all four Hearts, he's the Voltron power conduit.\n    93→- **Nocturnus (Bat):** Noble nephew to the Underdark vampire queen. Must kill his aunt (who gave her soul to Tiamat via bat-worship ascension). Mans Mountain's Zenith in final war.\n    94→\n    95→### Arc 4: Forest's Zenith (Death) — Machu\n    96→\n    97→**The Conflict:** The king of Machu is trapped in madness. Mordred/Ramses is the \"madness prisoner.\" The Forest's Zenith has already been partially plundered by Mordred (he had the Heart of the Wilde and used it to awaken the forest guardian — the big boss).\n    98→\n    99→**The Discovery:** After defeating the forest guardian, the Heart of the Wilde transports the ENTIRE party to V-MARDOV — inside the Death's Zenith — where they fight Lich Ned'zailok (Keeper of Death's Anchor).\n   100→\n   101→**The Resolution:** Must use a sun-reflecting mirror to access the Zenith. Free the king from madness. Bring peace to the star-crossed three:\n   102→- Xerxes's soul: held prisoner at the bottom of the Zenith\n   103→- Ramses's soul: in Daina's bag, must be returned to the phylactery for cleansing\n   104→- Sabine: appears in the flash as both bodies merge into a demi-lich\n   105→\n   106→**Player Arcs:**\n   107→- **Sprocket (Rabbit):** Vessel for Sabine Ra's return. Gains Sabine's cloak (luck feat: 3 rerolls/long rest), can channel necromancy, wields Anchor of Death in final fight.\n   108→- **Brigitte (Giant Elk):** Collects all 12 zodiac forms. Each grants psychic connection + animal form + ability to locate the zodiac being. Zodiac Locket at bottom of Tomb of the First Curator = unlimited shapeshifts + 2 Wisdom.\n   109→- **Varris (Doe):** Linked to Lich Ned'zailok in V-MARDOV. Sits in Forest's Zenith control room during final fight.\n   110→\n   111→---\n   112→\n   113→## The Prophecy Structure\n   114→\n   115→The DM uses a **layered prophecy system** — three prophecies of increasing specificity, delivered by different sources:\n   116→\n   117→1. **Ruth's Divination** (first, broadest): Establishes the stakes — anchors falling, hearts stolen, Vecna coming, blood star heralding war. Four tarot cards drawn: tiger (wind), forge (creation), holy knight (faith), family (progenitors).\n   118→\n   119→2. **The Hags' Divination** (second, nursery rhyme): \"Double bubble toil and trouble\" — confirms the first zenith (Forest) is already compromised, pestilence is being prepared with children's souls, anchors being lifted and hearts smuggled.\n   120→\n   121→3. **Mia's Divination** (third, specific/actionable): Four verses mapping exactly to four locations with specific conditions for resolution. This is the quest roadmap.\n   122→\n   123→**Design Intent:** Each prophecy is more actionable than the last. Ruth gives the cosmic scale, the Hags give the immediate threat, Mia gives the actual quest structure.\n   124→\n   125→---\n   126→\n   127→## The Final Battle (Guard'Daemon / Armageddon)\n   128→\n   129→**Setup:** V-MORDRED's invasion. The blood star falls. All four Zeniths must be held simultaneously.\n   130→\n   131→**Battle Stations:**\n   132→- Forest's Zenith: **Varris** (control room)\n   133→- Mountain's Zenith: **Nocturnus**\n   134→- Winter's Zenith: **Dallor**\n   135→- Harvest's Zenith: **Irethia**\n   136→\n   137→**Champions (Anchor wielders):**\n   138→- Anchor of Death (sword): **Sprocket**\n   139→- Anchor of War (hammer-axe-javelin): **Daina**\n   140→- Anchor of Pestilence (pan flute): **Randal**\n   141→- Anchor of Famine (Diadem of Dawn / one wish): **Tesca**\n   142→\n   143→**Power Conduit:**\n   144→- **Bergs** as Warforged powered by Heart of the Forge, channeling all four Hearts in a Voltron-style beam convergence\n   145→\n   146→**Heart Champions:**\n   147→- Arcane (Forge): Bergs\n   148→- Radiant (Faith): Stalwart\n   149→- Natural (Wilde): Brigitte\n   150→- Fae (Progenitors): Nyx\n   151→\n   152→---\n   153→\n   154→## Unused Ideas and Story Bites\n   155→\n   156→### The Colosseum City\n   157→A city that forces inhabitants to fight their way to the center circle. Citizens can only be saved by reaching the center and winning single combat. Could be a gladiatorial side-arc or the orc chieftain's domain.\n   158→\n   159→### The Dome\n   160→A giant dome of blackness, open at the top. Party must find a MacGuffin in the dark and make their way out. A dungeon concept — could be deployed anywhere.\n   161→\n   162→### The Underground Crime Network\n   163→- NPC tells party about \"the underworld\" — it's a trap\n   164→- Railcart getaway with bombs in back carts covering tracks\n   165→- The bombs are actually breaches for an invasion\n   166→- **THE PARTY IS FRAMED FOR THE INVASION**\n   167→- This is a fully-formed heist-gone-wrong arc\n   168→\n   169→### Miscellaneous Combat Ideas\n   170→- Misty Step Rogue: Grimnak\n   171→- Badass ally dimension doors in, saves the party, dimension doors out before they come to\n   172→- Battlefield with elemental planes and affinities (environmental combat mechanics)\n   173→\n   174→### The Silver Scales of Bahamut\n   175→Two philosophical factions within Bahamut's priesthood:\n   176→- Those who judge by **Justice** (what is fair by law)\n   177→- Those who judge by **Righteousness** (what is morally correct)\n   178→- The tension: \"All that is Just is not Righteous! All that is Righteous is not Just!\"\n   179→- This could fuel internal conflict within Conquis Abbey\n   180→\n   181→---\n   182→\n   183→## DM Design Patterns\n   184→\n   185→### Thematic Mirroring\n   186→Every level of the narrative mirrors the Vecna/Sabine dynamic:\n   187→- Cosmic: Vecna vs. Sabine (twin souls, jealousy)\n   188→- Mortal: Mordred's love for Sabine → corruption\n   189→- Player: Twin soul mechanics (Daina/Bergs, Nyx/Xyn)\n   190→- Structural: Material plane vs. V-MARDOV\n   191→\n   192→### The Monkey's Paw Principle\n   193→Nothing comes free. Every power has a cost:\n   194→- Magic items have hidden downsides\n   195→- The Diadem of Dawn grants a wish but removes spellcasting forever\n   196→- Nyx gains full warlock power at her sister's cost\n   197→- Bergs must give up his natural self to become Warforged\n   198→\n   199→### Player Agency Through Choice\n   200→The DM consistently designs climactic moments as binary choices:\n   201→- Nyx: End tieflings or rebuild the empire?\n   202→- Tesca: Use the one wish (and lose spellcasting) or find another way?\n   203→- Nocturnus: Kill his aunt or find another path?\n   204→- Each choice reshapes the world\n   205→\n   206→### Four-Part Symmetry\n   207→Everything comes in fours, matching the Horsemen:\n   208→- 4 Zeniths, 4 Hearts, 4 Anchors, 4 Horsemen\n   209→- 4 Eyes of Vecna (Green, Red, Blue, Orange)\n   210→- 4 Sources of Power (Arcane, Radiant, Natural, Fae)\n   211→- 12 players = 3 per Zenith\n   212→- 4 tarot cards in the first divination\n   213→\n   214→---\n   215→\n   216→## Key Lore Details for Adaptation\n   217→\n   218→### The Matrim Pact\n   219→An alliance of three peoples (humans, gnomes, half-elves) who fled the destruction of Uma on the back of the giant turtle Maturin. They built a hidden city — a buried pyramid — that serves as the Harvest's Zenith. The turtle's revival is one of the campaign's most visually spectacular set pieces.\n   220→\n   221→### Tygras and the Tieflings\n   222→Tygras was a tiefling warlord who made a pact with a fae demon. His superweapon destroyed Uma, creating the desert. The Covenants of Magic were created in response. Tygras was sealed in the Harvest's Zenith where he's been feeding off its energy ever since. His legacy: all tieflings are his descendants through fae corruption.\n   223→\n   224→### The Watchful Order\n   225→The magical government/police force. They determine which magic is legal and which creatures live or die. They're a potential ally or obstacle depending on the party's methods.\n   226→\n   227→### Lord Jerefor\n   228→A smuggler in Argos — the suspected smuggler of the Hearts (\"hearts are smuggled\" from the second prophecy).\n   229→\n   230→### The Blood Star\n   231→A recurring astronomical omen. First appeared when Tygras came to power. Will appear again for Guard'Daemon. It physically falls — a meteorite. The last one killed King Thorson Bronzebeard II.\n   232→\n   233→### Sabine Ra\n   234→Will return as a demi-lich through the merging of Ramses and Xerxes in the Forest's Zenith. Sprocket serves as her vessel. She is simultaneously goddess of life, death, and fate — the most powerful entity in the setting, but shattered and scattered.\n   235→\n   236→---\n   237→\n   238→## Map of the Five Key Points\n   239→\n   240→From the Thornberry Shade's map (bubbled up in five places):\n   241→1. **Var'taos Ruins / Valley of Anchors** — Origin of magic\n   242→2. **Vag Mahar** — Dwarven city, Mountain's Zenith\n   243→3. **Machu** — Forest's Zenith\n   244→4. **Bynar** — Winter's Zenith\n   245→5. **Tygras Logath** — Harvest's Zenith (additionally had a burning ember)\n   246→\n   247→These five points form the geographic skeleton of the entire campaign.\n   248→\n   249→"}]},"uuid":"588bc790-7851-45b9-96f9-de9b3b9f0aa2","timestamp":"2026-03-19T05:59:04.530Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"e2b9d830-1806-4460-bb70-bfe902d0fbb4","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/eviemini/Documents/code/server-manager","sessionId":"e89c0432-c0b0-471a-97e7-f75f9aa647b4","version":"2.1.78","gitBranch":"main","slug":"stateful-seeking-melody"}

{"parentUuid":"848db453-56b3-4fa3-95f3-2b8dae58b668","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"616419db-f8ea-4cbf-8b1b-8e3d3d602562","agentId":"a7319d0ecd9725385","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01CYZabdnwzo3zybqkTogsJt","type":"tool_result","content":"     1→---\n     2→type: character\n     3→role: pc\n     4→name: \"Varis Laindon\"\n     5→aliases: [\"Varis Liadon\", \"Knight of the Dusken Wood\", \"The Eyes\"]\n     6→race: \"Half-Elf\"\n     7→class: \"Ranger\"\n     8→affiliation: \"[[Scarlet Tunic Brigade]]\"\n     9→location: \"Mardov (traveling)\"\n    10→status: alive\n    11→first_appearance: \"Chapter 6\"\n    12→source: [\"Player Notes (Ch. 6-136)\", \"Varis's Backstory\", \"Summary 28\"]\n    13→art: [\"assets/pc-art-source/Varis/Varis Laindon.jpg\", \"assets/pc-art-source/Varis/Varis Laindon -- Knight of the Dusken Wood.jpg\"]\n    14→---\n    15→\n    16→## Description\n    17→\n    18→Varis Laindon is the party's sentinel -- a half-elf ranger who fights because he could not save his father, protects because he could not protect his village, and keeps watch because if he stops watching, it happens again. He arrived through a portal from [[vodram]], an alternate reality destroyed by [[Vecna]], and carries that apocalyptic weight with quiet, devastating competence. His 200+ damage single-turn is the culmination of every sunrise he watched and every watch he kept.\n    19→\n    20→## Appearance & Costume\n    21→\n    22→### Standard Form\n    23→A **muscular half-elf male** with **golden-brown shoulder-length hair** and slightly pointed ears marking his elven heritage. His face is ruggedly handsome with a **short beard and stubble**, strong jawline, and watchful brown eyes that never stop moving -- tracking, assessing, counting.\n    24→\n    25→He wears **brown leather armor** with ornate **gold-copper trim** -- scrollwork designs embossed into the chest piece and shoulder guards. **Fur-lined shoulders and cloak** give him a ranger's woodsman silhouette, warm and practical. Multiple **leather pouches** hang from his belt. His overall palette is earthy: brown leather, gold-copper accents, green forest behind him.\n    26→\n    27→He holds a **longbow** at the ready, with **glowing cyan-teal magical arrows** that emit an ethereal light. A full **quiver** is visible on his back. The cyan arrow glow is his visual signature -- when Varis draws his bow, cyan light reflects in his eyes and the world goes still.\n    28→\n    29→### Knight of the Dusken Wood Form\n    30→A weathered, grizzled transformation. His hair has shifted to **long silver-white**, his face more lined and hardened. He wears a **dark green-brown cloak** over leather armor, firing his longbow mid-combat in a **misty forest** setting. The palette shifts from warm golds to cool greens and greys. He moves like fog between trees -- visible, then not, then there is an arrow where you were standing.\n    31→\n    32→## Personality\n    33→\n    34→**ISTJ (The Logistician)** with a buried INFJ core. Varis presents as practical and duty-bound. He organizes defenses, commands guards, makes tactical assessments. But beneath the military bearing is someone who watches sunrises alone, carries a pseudo-dragon skull as a talisman, and dumps diamonds on dying strangers without being asked.\n    35→\n    36→**Motivations**: Protect the weak from those with power and without conscience. Find Yeldan -- or the truth of what happened to his father. Secretly, he wants to believe his father is alive.\n    37→\n    38→**Under pressure**: Becomes terrifyingly competent. Voice goes flat, movements become economical. Channels the Knight of the Dusken Wood -- silver-haired, battle-hardened, a figure out of legend.\n    39→\n    40→**Moral compass**: Hates petty politics with visceral fury. Will always fight for those \"screwed over by petty politics.\" Will break rules for righteousness but never for convenience.\n    41→\n    42→## Backstory\n    43→\n    44→Varis grew up at [[Tower Bridge]], a wilderness outpost near [[dawnpass]]. His father Yeldan was a hunter, scout, and guide; his mother Lianala a druid. At age 95 (roughly 17 in human years), they hunted a pack of pseudo-dragons together. He kept the skull of his first kill.\n    45→\n    46→Two weeks later, Yeldan spotted undead scouts. The local lord believed him but could not convince others in time. Two more weeks, and the army arrived -- zombies, skeletons, wraiths, mummies, led by the lich [[Ned'Zailok]]. Varis and his father volunteered for the militia.\n    47→\n    48→They held for a week. Yeldan was sent as the fastest scout to request aid. The militia broke a day later. The [[Scarlet Tunic Brigade]] arrived -- too late to hold, fast enough to cover retreat. Most civilians escaped. The militia took massive losses. Yeldan was never found.\n    49→\n    50→STB commander [[dockson]] shamed the lord into acting. Varis enlisted with the STB, fighting alongside them for eighteen months until Ned'zailok was defeated. He has been protecting the weak and keeping an ear out for the lich ever since.\n    51→\n    52→## Key Moments\n    53→\n    54→| Chapter | Event | Significance |\n    55→|---------|-------|-------------|\n    56→| 6 | Appears through a portal from [[vodram]] | A member of the STB from an alternate reality destroyed by Vecna |\n    57→| 28 | Vodram mission with [[Brigit Wildfire]], [[Nyx Khamestria]], [[Irethia]] | \"These people from Sabine's world... they got dropped into the worst place possible, and never complained\" |\n    58→| 28 | Kills Roderick in cold blood; Brigit sees and still trusts him | The moment Varis learned that trust can survive witnessing violence |\n    59→| 29 | Secret collaboration with Brigit to find hobgoblin Grinda | Brigit covers for him in wolf form |\n    60→| 100+ | Commands city guards, inspires former-drab to take up arms | Grows from ranger following orders to commander giving them |\n    61→| 136 | Fires the shot that reverberates through a god's body | 200+ damage. The culmination of every sunrise he watched. |\n    62→\n    63→## Relationships\n    64→\n    65→- **[[Brigit Wildfire]]** -- The Forest Bond. Fellow protectors of the wild, cosmologically linked through [[Stag Zodiac]] and [[Doe Zodiac]]. The campaign's most harmonious pairing.\n    66→- **[[Nocturnus Wayne]]** -- Varis brought Nocturnus back from death with a diamond. Unspoken bond.\n    67→- **[[Sprocket Overspark]]** -- Both assigned to the [[Forest Zenith]] with Brigit. Elk, Doe, Rabbit.\n    68→- **[[Irethia]]** -- Vodram mission squad. Mutual respect forged through extreme danger in another plane.\n    69→- **Yeldan** -- His father. Never found. Varis stopped expecting to hear news but never stopped hoping.\n    70→- **[[Ned'Zailok]]** -- The lich who destroyed his home. Can never be certain a lich is gone.\n    71→\n    72→## Fighting Style\n    73→\n    74→**Class**: Ranger (Hunter).\n    75→\n    76→**Primary Stats**: DEX > WIS > CON.\n    77→\n    78→**Weapons**: Longbow with magical cyan-teal arrows (primary); short sword (backup).\n    79→\n    80→**Signature abilities**:\n    81→- **The Draw**: His signature moment. When Varis pulls his bow, everything slows. Breathing steadies, pupils contract, body perfectly aligned. Cyan glow reflects in his eyes. Release. Arrow flies true.\n    82→- **Multi-Attack**: In sustained combat, fires volleys of arrows with devastating speed.\n    83→- **Colossus Slayer / Hunter's Mark**: Bonus damage that stacks to 200+ in critical moments.\n    84→- **Natural Explorer**: Moves through forests like fog between trees.\n    85→\n    86→**Combat philosophy**: Military precision. He sees the battlefield from above (metaphorically), identifies targets, and calls them. His fighting style is vigilance weaponized -- every watch he kept, every sunrise he guarded, condensed into the moment the arrow leaves the string.\n    87→\n    88→## Visual Description (Session 100 Commission)\n    89→\n    90→*From the Audrey commission for \"Mardov: Echoes of the Shadowfell\" (Session 100, April 2022)*\n    91→\n    92→- **Race/Class**: Elf Ranger\n    93→- **Player**: Scott\n    94→- **Key Features**: Classic elf ranger in garb and build; does NOT have long flowing blonde hair -- instead has straw yellow/red hair cropped into a practical cut; exposed forearm with stag horn tattoos up and down it like vines (tattoo transforms into his bow)\n    95→- **Equipment**:\n    96→  - Tattoo-turned-bow with lightning motif, glows electric blue\n    97→  - Breastplate made of bronze dragon scales\n    98→  - Ring of air elemental command\n    99→- **Art direction**: \"Scott always scoffs at the pictures I show him that have the long flowing blonde hair\"\n   100→\n   101→## Gallery\n   102→\n   103→![[assets/pc-art-source/Varis/Varis Laindon.jpg]]\n   104→![[assets/pc-art-source/Varis/Varis Laindon -- Knight of the Dusken Wood.jpg]]\n   105→"}]},"uuid":"08e6bb57-d1f8-4197-8b51-04db9797d1ac","timestamp":"2026-03-19T05:59:17.254Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"848db453-56b3-4fa3-95f3-2b8dae58b668","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/eviemini/Documents/code/server-manager","sessionId":"e89c0432-c0b0-471a-97e7-f75f9aa647b4","version":"2.1.78","gitBranch":"main","slug":"stateful-seeking-melody"}

{"parentUuid":"8a0f431c-db49-432c-b7f5-edaecf427e95","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"616419db-f8ea-4cbf-8b1b-8e3d3d602562","agentId":"a7319d0ecd9725385","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01K88CUuPrWU9SQHQPmABfG2","type":"tool_result","content":"     1→---\n     2→type: source\n     3→category: backstory\n     4→character: \"[[Varis Liadon]]\"\n     5→source: \"Backstories/Varys' backstory.txt\"\n     6→---\n     7→\n     8→# Varis Liadon — Backstory\n     9→\n    10→Wilderness outpost childhood. Dad ([[Yeldan Liadon|Yeldan]]) was a hunter, woodsman, guide, scout, and messenger. Mom ([[lianala]]) is a Druid. The city was [[Tower Bridge]], a village outside [[dawnpass]] and [[Eavallan Forest]] — elves and humans alike live there.\n    11→\n    12→Started hunting and learning from both parents. Last big hunt was when [[Varis Liadon|Varis]] was 95 (approximately 17 for a human) — a pack of pseudo dragons that migrated through and killed sheep. After a successful hunt, parents told him to keep a trophy. He kept the skull of his first kill of the pack (tiny enough to be a trinket or piece of jewelry).\n    13→\n    14→2 weeks later, dad spotted undead scouts. The local lord believed him, but couldn't convince farther people (at least, in time). 2 more weeks, and the army arrived — zombies, skeletons, wraiths, mummies — led and created by the lich [[Ned'Zailok]].\n    15→\n    16→Volunteered with dad for militia, and took an adult name at the time. Fought beside dad, and local militia held for a week, hoping for relief from outside. Attacks got worse and supplies got limited after a week. Dad was sent (fastest scout) with trophies to request aid fast.\n    17→\n    18→They broke a day later. The [[Scarlet Tunic Brigade]] arrived at the same time — too late to hold, but fast enough to cover a retreat and evac. Most of the non-militia got out, including mom. Militia took massive losses when it broke. Never found dad, and he never arrived at the lord's seat.\n    19→\n    20→[[dockson]] — an honorable paladin, high-ranking [[Scarlet Tunic Brigade|STB]] general type — shamed the lord into acting. Looked like he would have challenged him to a duel (or just killed him in his throne room) if he didn't cave.\n    21→\n    22→Militia kept fighting with [[Scarlet Tunic Brigade|STB]] until [[Ned'Zailok]] was defeated — which took another 18 months. Most dispersed after. Mom stayed in or near a larger city when the fight ended. [[Varis Liadon|Varis]] enlisted with [[Scarlet Tunic Brigade|STB]], hoping to find more about dad. Never did.\n    23→\n    24→Been trying to help protect the weak and those screwed over by petty politics with the [[Scarlet Tunic Brigade|STB]] ever since (a few years). Always keeps an ear out for word that [[Ned'Zailok]] has resurfaced. Can't ever be sure a lich is gone. Also keeps an ear out for any news of [[Yeldan Liadon|Yeldan]], but stopped expecting to hear anything not long after the end of that war.\n    25→\n    26→## Key Characters\n    27→\n    28→- [[Yeldan Liadon]] — Father, missing (presumed dead)\n    29→- [[lianala]] — Mother, druid\n    30→- [[Ned'Zailok]] — Lich who destroyed his homeland\n    31→- [[dockson]] — STB paladin who shamed the local lord into action\n    32→- [[Scarlet Tunic Brigade]] — The organization Varis joined\n    33→- [[Tower Bridge]] — Childhood home\n    34→- [[Eavallan Forest]] — Nearby elven forest\n    35→"}]},"uuid":"b42ee5be-54b7-4271-aafa-70f6a868ba02","timestamp":"2026-03-19T05:59:17.756Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"8a0f431c-db49-432c-b7f5-edaecf427e95","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/eviemini/Documents/code/server-manager","sessionId":"e89c0432-c0b0-471a-97e7-f75f9aa647b4","version":"2.1.78","gitBranch":"main","slug":"stateful-seeking-melody"}

Wells|Dr. M.B. Wells]], Stalin Little, and [[Dallor Dallorbill]]).
