{"parentUuid":"31074844-d556-4d62-85cb-02d109bd5920","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"616419db-f8ea-4cbf-8b1b-8e3d3d602562","agentId":"a7319d0ecd9725385","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01K9nLgymkiyTutFPNfUBBzU","type":"tool_result","content":"     1→---\n     2→type: dm-idea\n     3→title: \"story arch ideas\"\n     4→source: [\"NFS/Ideas/story arch ideas.txt\"]\n     5→category: idea\n     6→tags: [idea]\n     7→---\n     8→\n     9→notes for overarching story:\n    10→\n    11→The forrest's zenith contains the anchor of death magic to this world, the desert's zenith holds the life magic anchor once both are severed the avatar of the raven queen wil cease to exist, \n    12→sabine's crystaline heart will be removed from the adventurer's and they will no longer be under threat of being spirited away off to V-MORDRED's universe. \n    13→\n    14→this is the only way to force V-MORDRED back into the realm from which he came. \n    15→The anchors are a pair of swords thrust in the center of each zenith. \n    16→\n    17→Death's Anchor: \n    18→\tis a magic sword which has been thrust in the forest's zenith since time memorium. The sword has long since been turned to obsidian through the test of time, \n    19→\tthough legend tells of its black steel blade, the Anchor now looks to be made of black glass. \n    20→\t\n    21→\tWhenever you roll a 1-5 on its attack roll, a lingering curse inside it lashes out at you dealing 4D10 force damage to you, you may make a charisma save (DC 15) for half damage. \n    22→\tIf you spend a long rest with the blade, you may make a charisma check (DC 20). \n    23→\tOn a failure the curse deals 2D10 force damage to you and until your next long rest the cursed blade gains no bonus to attack or damage rolls. \n    24→\tOn a success you gain control over the curse until your next long rest where you lose control of it again. \n    25→\tWhile in control of the curse bonus to attack and damage rolls increases to 2+ and the curse can't lash out at you. Also the cursed blade has 10 charges. \n    26→\tWhen you succeed on a attack roll with the cursed blade, you may expend charges to activate the curse on the target, when you do choose a curse effect: \n    27→\n    28→\tDamage curse: \n    29→\t\tdeal an extra 2D10 force damage to the target, they may make a charisma save (DC 15) for half damage. Charge cost: 1.\n    30→\tWeakening curse: \n    31→\t\tthe target makes a charisma save (DC 15), on a failure the target becomes poisoned, has 1- on attack and damage rolls, and has 2- to AC for 1 hour. \n    32→\t\tThe target may repeat the saving throw each turn, ending the effects on a success unless you spend a charge each turn to prevent them. {"parentUuid":"d84c3429-1380-41a7-ba18-60c511408a32","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"616419db-f8ea-4cbf-8b1b-8e3d3d602562","agentId":"a7319d0ecd9725385","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01B8Ryttq9HPFUoiMvdGFCeF","type":"tool_result","content":"     1→---\n     2→type: dm-note\n     3→title: \"Zodiacs and Player Symbolism\"\n     4→source: [\"NFS/Notes/Zodiacs and Player Symbolism.txt\"]\n     5→category: \"\"\n     6→tags: []\n     7→---\n     8→\n     9→\n    10→Player:\n    11→------------------------------------------------- FORREST's ZENITH && ANCHOR OF DEATH && HEART OF THE WILDE -------------------------------------------------\n    12→01) SPROCKET :    Represents College of Mages, Closest representative of Raven Queen,    rabbit, death is going to bring about SABINE's RETURN as she is the goddess of fate, he will serve as the vessel by which SABINE RA the demi-lich will rise. This will happen sometime during or after the cleansing of both RAMSES and XERSES souls inside the FOREST's ZENITH, will grant him the ability to wear her cloak, the cloak grants luck feat which allows him to roll 3 die rolls a long rest as well as channel necromancy well enough to wield the ANCHOR OF DEATH in the final fight. \n    13→02) BRIGITTE :    Represents the magic of nature, ties to the zodiacs, EAVALLAN     , giant elk, going to be the one to figure out that every zodiac she sees will grant her their form, when in that form she has a psychic connection with the zodiac that it represents, she can find them as well as communicate with the zodiac, collect all 12 and she is able to attune to the ZODIAC LOCKET which is at the bottom of the TOMB OF THE FIRST CURATOR, ZODIAC LOCKET will open access to the HEART OF THE WILD with that power source the wearer is granted unlimited shapeshifts, granted that there is a cooldown period of 1m (will store a shapeshift to take over AFTER the druid has spent all her wildshapes, grants +2 wisdom as well)\n    14→03) VARRIS   :    Represents the darkest timeline, tied to FOREST's ZENITH          ,       doe, tied to the DEATH ZENITH<as its known on the other side> by the lich who resides there, Lich Ned'zailok -- Keeper of Death's Anchor, when the party beats the final baddie of the zenith, the HEART OF THE WILDE will pop off the beast and transport the whole party to V-MARDOV... INSIDE the DEATH's ZENITH, there they will fight the lich and steal his anchor of death. <HEART OF THE WILDE on our side, ANCHOR OF DEATH on their side>. Will need to sit in the control room for the FOREST'S ZENITH at the final fight. \n    15→\n    16→------------------------------------------------- MOUNTAIN's ZENITH && ANCHOR OF WAR && HEART OF THE FORGE -------------------------------------------------\n    17→04) DAINA    :    Represents THE MOUNTAIN's ZENITH, father is the current forgelord , wolverine, heiress of the FIRST FORGE, needs to embrace the twin soul, when you find your twin your power increases (increase the stat that your combined bonuses has the most of, for example if Daina and Bergs have +3 to strength ... chances are thats the stat that they both have the most of, it gets +1 when they are together if they train), she must wield the power of the ANCHOR OF WAR <a half hammer-half axe, half javilin> in order to harness the FIRST FLAME to help transform BERGS\n    18→05) BERGS    :    Represents the ARTIFICER's GUILD, twin soul with DAINA            ,      wolf, needs to give up wild part of self (natural) and embrace change, heir to the FIRST FORGE, this will happen as the pair is granted the power to wield the HEART OF THE FORGE, this will be the power source for the WARFORGED that BERGS must become. By wielding the HEART OF THE FORGE within him, BERGS will be insanely powerful when combined with the powers of the other 3 hearts <voltron beams style>., \n    19→06) NOCTURNUS:    Represents Nobility, nephew to the queen of the underdark         ,       bat, will be insturmental in preventing an invasion of the overlands, must follow phrophecy to kill his AUNT, will be the one to man the MOUNTAIN's ZENITH in the final war. Aunt is a vampire, given her soul to TIAMAT in order to complete an ascention ritual by worshipping the bat. \n    20→\n    21→------------------------------------------------- WINTER's ZENITH && ANCHOR OF PESTILLENCE && HEART OF THE FAITH -------------------------------------------------\n    22→07) RANDAL   :    Represents the College of bards,                                  ,    turtle, needs to get the panflute and needs to resurrect the turtle with it (its inside the serpent), RANDAL will need to use the panflute to wield the ANCHOR OF PESTILENCE which is the turtles skeleton, by resurrecting MIA with the panflute, her skeleton is going to be given flesh, she will surface and in return she will grant a tune on the panflute which IS THE ANCHOR OF PESTILLENCE, turns out its hella magic and there are a number of spells it wields, one of which is the anti-inspiration (half the value of positive inspiration can take away from one of an enemies roll), or the group inspiration which grants it to the whole party, they know that finding the SUNLESS CITADEL in the Shifting Sands will point the way to finding the ANCHOR OF PESTILLENCE\n    23→08) STALWART :    Represents the Faith, worships revenge, tied to SUNLESS CITADAEL  ,  mongoose, going to discover that the Heart of the Faith has been stolen by a White Dragon, however his drive for revenge will lead him to discover that the ANCHOR OF PESTILLENCE originates from the SUNLESS CITADEL (A Yuan-ti fortress hidden amongst the shifing Sands) the irony here being that there isnt a winter's zenith for STALWART to find at the Yaun-ti fortress, instead he will find a SHIELD OF RETRIBUTION which when combined with the HEART OF THE FAITH will allow him to channel Divine Magic without being tied to a diety, deliverying retribution to the great serpent for his crimes against the turtle and winter's curators will break the curse (also delivering the heart of the faith to CONQUIS ABBEY) will fix it, the WINTER's ZENITH is within the mountian of BYNAR which is guarded by the Winter Dragon, CURATORS are actually a ruined tribe because the YUAN-TI had stolen the ANCHOR OF PESTILLENCE which is currently missing, \n    24→09) DALLOR   :    Represents greed and a desire to avoid ones own fate              ,   serpent, going to be the one to determine that the SUNLESS CITADEL is actually a shrine to the Serpent's Chosen and original YUAN-TI, this ascended will call the serpent from the waters to defend her. Will be the one to eventually man the WINTER'S Zenith in the last battle. \n    25→\n    26→------------------------------------------------- HARVEST's ZENITH && ANCHOR OF FAMINE && HEART OF THE PROGENITORS -------------------------------------------------\n    27→10) NYX      :    Represents the FAE, FAE-TOUCHED, twin souls with her sister       ,       owl, going to discover why tieflings exist <her patron's last patronee was TYGRAS>, her sister is her twin soul, when she was born she had a syst which was her sister, that is the soul that the arch-fey took in exchange for her powers, her parents were tricked into having another daughter, this one he put the soul in and controlled XYN, this is going to uncover the HARVEST's ZENITH, which is the seat from which TYGRAS was controlling her sister. Will wield the HEART OF THE PROGENITOR's which will be infused in her spellbook and grant her access to ALL warlock rituals, not just the ones she's learned, will allow her to channel raw fae magics, can become an aasimar if shed like\n    28→11) IRETHIA  :    Represents the FAE-TOUCHED people trapped in FAE by MIA and TYGRAS,       fox, going to discover the difference between AASIMAR and TIEFLINGS, will be the one to wield the HARVEST's ZENITH in the final battle\n    29→12) TESCA    :    Represents Blind Faith, must find the DIADEM OF DAWN,             ,     mouse, going to discover that the DIADEM of DAWN is actually the ANCHOR of FAMINE, which is an artifact which grants the wielder one wish while removing their spellcasting ability forever after the cast, this power will be needed to truely destroy TYGRAS's FAE-MACHINE which is still in tact.  \n    30→\n    31→\n    32→\n    33→\n    34→\n    35→\n    36→\n    37→* NOTES:\n    38→\tTwin souls:\n    39→\t\t- VECNA and SABINE were the ORIGINAL TWIN SOULS! thats why they share a domain. however SABINE is also the goddess of life as well. \n    40→\t\t- VECNA was always jealous of his sisters additional powers and vowed that he would take them from her\n    41→\t\t- the whole campaign revolves around twin souls because the goddess and god of death were also twin souls\n    42→\n    43→\tFate:\n    44→\t\t- SABINE as goddess of life and death is also the goddess of fate, as her powers are pre-ordained. your time comes when it comes. \n    45→\n    46→\tSources of Power:\n    47→\t\t- Arcane (HEART OF THE FORGE):\n    48→\t\t\t- champion = BERGS     : body will literally become arcane energy, by being re-forged his arcane power will more than double, affording himself a \n    49→\t\t- Radiant (HEART OF THE FAITH):\n    50→\t\t\t- champion = STALWART  : uses SHIELD OF RETRIBUTION to channel divine power (raw) without a diety, instead choosing to harness the same source of their power\n    51→\t\t- Natural (HEART OF THE WILDE):\n    52→\t\t\t- champion = BRIGITTE  : uses the ZODIAC LOCKET to channel the cosmic ZODIAC powers (source of druidcraft)\n    53→\t\t- Fae/Warlock (HEART OF THE PROGENITORS):\n    54→\t\t\t- champion = NYX      : going to use the influence of her patron to enslave and master her patron in order to grant herself full access to her powers at the cost of her sisters\n    55→"}]},"uuid":"ba68cbf0-802f-49b2-b7a2-f3db646697d2","timestamp":"2026-03-19T05:59:36.251Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"d84c3429-1380-41a7-ba18-60c511408a32","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/eviemini/Documents/code/server-manager","sessionId":"e89c0432-c0b0-471a-97e7-f75f9aa647b4","version":"2.1.78","gitBranch":"main","slug":"stateful-seeking-melody"}

Drought has destroyed the Harvestlands. An orc chieftain, corrupted by the same fae demon that empowered Tygras, is besieging DawnPass.\n/Users/eviemini/Documents/code/server-manager/mardov-vault/sources/lore-extraction/story-arc-dm-notes.md-51-\nmardov-vault/sources/lore-extraction/story-arc-dm-notes.md:52:**The Discovery:** The resurgence of orc sorcerers matches to the day with the last time it rained — supernatural famine. The Matrim Pact (humans, gnomes, half-elves united on the Maturin Turtle) holds the key.\n/Users/eviemini/Documents/code/server-manager/mardov-vault/sources/lore-extraction/story-arc-dm-notes.md-53-\nmardov-vault/sources/lore-extraction/story-arc-dm-notes.md-54-**The Dungeon:** The Harvest's Zenith is inside a buried pyramid (the great ziggurat), where Tygras himself still exists as an arcane being, feeding off the Zenith's internal energies.\n/Users/eviemini/Documents/code/server-manager/mardov-vault/sources/lore-extraction/story-arc-dm-notes.md-55-\nmardov-vault/sources/lore-extraction/story-arc-dm-notes.md-56-**The Choice:** After destroying Tygras's echo, Nyx meets the fae demon. She inherits the Mark of Tygras (Sable's Beacon). She can either rid the world of \"demon children\" (end tieflings) or rebuild the lost empire by adding tieflings to the Matrim Pact.\n/Users/eviemini/Documents/code/server-manager/mardov-vault/sources/lore-extraction/story-arc-dm-notes.md-57-\n--\nmardov-vault/sources/lore-extraction/story-arc-dm-notes.md-66-- **Irethia (Fox):** Discovers Aasimar vs. Tiefling distinction. Mans the Harvest Zenith in final battle.\nmardov-vault/sources/lore-extraction/story-arc-dm-notes.md-67-- **Tesca (Mouse):** Discovers the Diadem of Dawn IS the Anchor of Famine — one wish that permanently removes spellcasting. Each character's journey is tracked chronologically through party splits, reunions, and pivotal moments.\n    10→    10→\n    11→    11→> **Confidence Legend**: [HIGH] = directly from player notes or handouts. [MEDIUM] = inferred from structural evidence. [LOW] = reconstructed from context.\n    12→    12→\n    13→    13→---\n    14→    14→\n    15→    15→## BRIGIT (Wild Spirit of Fire)\n    16→    16→\n    17→    17→**Player**: Liz\n    18→    18→**Class**: Druid (Circle of the Moon) / Barbarian multiclass\n    19→    19→**Race**: Changeling/Shapeshifter\n    20→    20→**Key Items**: Anchor of Death (locket), Stag Zodiac\n    21→    21→**Track**: Tieflings -> Distillery -> Good Guys Guild\n    22→    22→\n    23→    23→### Arc Summary\n    24→    24→\n    25→    25→Brigit begins as a chaotic, multi-formed druid struggling with the many voices in her head. She becomes a Progenitor through a ritual in Eden with the Children of the Forest, gains custody of the Anchor of Death in her locket, and carries the Stag Zodiac. Her arc is one of identity -- learning to channel her rage and multiplicity into purpose. She is the Wild Spirit of Fire, and through her shapeshifting, serves as the party's most versatile combatant and emotional anchor. By Act 4 she has ascended the locket, lost her elemental powers during the Harvest Zenith battle, and must find her way forward without her primary coping mechanism.\n    26→    26→\n    27→    27→### {"parentUuid":"4bf25a82-519c-4849-9e24-206c865b83e3","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"616419db-f8ea-4cbf-8b1b-8e3d3d602562","agentId":"a7319d0ecd9725385","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_014kGSNh8VDBBmoT9m66h6Q5","type":"tool_result","content":"   160→---\n   161→\n   162→## VARIS\n   163→\n   164→**Player**: (unknown)\n   165→**Class**: Ranger (with portal/planar abilities via crystalline heart)\n   166→**Race**: Elf (Eladrin connection)\n   167→**Key Items**: Curator's Plea (bow), Horn (weapon), Ring of Air Elemental\n   168→**Track**: Tieflings -> Distillery -> Good Guys Guild\n   169→\n   170→### Arc Summary\n   171→\n   172→Varis is the party's ranger and moral compass -- a survivor of Vodram's fall who carries deep guilt about those he couldn't save. His crystalline heart grants him portal abilities, and his connection to the Autumn Court through Reynardine defines his political role. He wields the Curator's Plea and the Horn, channeling the legacy of fallen civilizations. His arc is about duty, the weight of command, and finding purpose after loss. By Act 4, he becomes Knight of Autumn and bears an almost unbearable burden of responsibility.\n   173→\n   174→### Timeline\n   175→\n   176→| Chapter | Event | Significance |\n   177→|---------|-------|-------------|\n   178→| 0-41 | United party; survivor of Vodram | [MEDIUM] Foundation |\n   179→| 49T | Cloud City: goes invisible to infiltrate, encounters various NPCs at the tavern (Sam, Matthew Windsmith, Itori) | [HIGH] Diplomatic/intelligence role |\n   180→| 64T | Taste-tests brewing but fails (too drunk), meets beige ninja (air genasi) | [HIGH] Character moment |\n   181→| 69T | Fights Necromancer: 77-damage critical with lightning bowstring, goes down twice from Finger of Death | [HIGH] Nearly dies fighting Ned'Zailok |\n   182→| 69T | Vision: sees himself in a tower with the Necromancer by a fire; spirit of stag enters | [HIGH] Mystical connection to antagonist |\n   183→| 72T-76T | Tournament: enters Mage's Prison, nearly killed by Morkoths, rescued by Randall | [HIGH] Vulnerable moment |\n   184→| 78T | Beholder trial: bow destroyed by Death Ray, fights with sword and chain lightning | [HIGH] Adapts to loss |\n   185→| 79T | Feywilde: recognized as autumn emissary, sacrifices blood in the vote, feels duty hit \"like a ton of bricks\" | [HIGH] Seasonal court identity |\n   186→| 82T | Votes in 6th trial, teaches funeral customs | [MEDIUM] Cultural role |\n   187→| 83T | Buys 2 superior, 2 greater, 12 regular healing potions; sets up refugee operations at keep | [HIGH] Logistics |\n   188→| 85T | Arrives at Distillery via Kuo-Toa boat (sent by Reynardine), praised as hero on arrival | [HIGH] Homecoming |\n   189→| 86 | Reunion: Guardian of Nature form, teaches funeral customs for fallen, builds memorial | [HIGH] Leadership |\n   190→| 87T | Organizes keep operations, discovers portals in wells and at Sprocket's tower | [HIGH] Security operations |\n   191→| 88-89 | Takes stock of damage, mentors Iris (promoted to Countess), rebuilds keep | [HIGH] Commander role |\n   192→| 90 | Uses zombies as necromantic detectors, discovers portal to autumn feywilde | [HIGH] Tactical innovation |\n   193→| 100-103 | Shadowfell and reunion adventures | [MEDIUM] Continuing journey |\n   194→| 116 | Convergence and Betrayal | [HIGH] Major plot event |\n   195→| 125 | Retrieves final Asmodeus seal | [HIGH] Endgame quest completion |\n   196→| 127-128 | Conquis Abbey: phases out of world to avoid sorcerers, channels Horn and Curator's Plea, chains lightning, kills sorcerers with force shots through throats | [HIGH] Peak combat performance |\n   197→| 128 | \"In the back: I have witnessed the fall of Vodram... through me their legacy endures\" -- hears Curator's Plea speak | [HIGH] Legacy connection deepens |\n   198→| 128 | Contacted by Gabriel/Reynardine during Oblivion experience: \"I can't believe you're here. You came to save me.\" | [HIGH] Spiritual connection |\n   199→| 130 | Cloud City: follows Keelick to protect him, casts Protection from Evil and Good on the king | [HIGH] Protective instincts |\n   200→| 130 | Takes guardian of nature form, erupts feral scream \"go home and protect your families,\" uses windwall to disperse mob | [HIGH] Leadership under pressure |\n   201→| 131 | Shoots Gabriel with Nautilus railgun: 200+ HP damage | [HIGH] Critical shot |\n   202→| 132 | Stone Table: fires Curator's Plea at Grat'z (CRIT), steps onto autumn plinth, accepts burden of duty (falls unconscious) | [HIGH] Embraces destiny |\n   203→| 133 | Becomes Knight of the Feywilde under Bergs; duty hits and he falls unconscious again, healed by Josh | [HIGH] New knighthood |\n   204→| 133 | Pledges to Bergs first among the knights | [HIGH] Loyalty |\n   205→| 135 | Divinity: casts Haste on self, Freedom of Movement from Brigit; fires at Tiamat with sharpshooter, 200+ damage CRIT | [HIGH] Extraordinary damage output |\n   206→| 135 | Casts Revivify on Nocturnus after Tiamat splits him in half | [HIGH] Saves critical ally |\n   207→| 136 | Fires massive shots at Tiamat; messages Kasim to prepare Claudere for war | [HIGH] Strategic commander |\n   208→| 136 | Goes down from Tiamat's fire breath; saved by turtle with healing potion | [HIGH] Near death again |\n   209→\n   210→### Turning Points\n   211→\n   212→1. **Vodram's Fall** (backstory): Defines his survivor's guilt and drive.\n   213→2. **Bow Destroyed (Ch 78T)**: Forced to adapt; eventually receives Reynardine's new bow.\n   214→3. **Autumn Plinth (Ch 132)**: Embraces his destiny as autumn knight, burden nearly kills him.\n   215→4. **200+ Damage Crits (Ch 135)**: Peak combat -- proves he's the party's deadliest striker.\n   216→\n   217→### Status (as of Ch 136)\n   218→\n   219→- Alive, Knight of the Feywilde (Autumn)"}]},"uuid":"e367e617-f6a0-4f76-93de-9afa780dca39","timestamp":"2026-03-19T06:00:00.429Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"4bf25a82-519c-4849-9e24-206c865b83e3","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/eviemini/Documents/code/server-manager","sessionId":"e89c0432-c0b0-471a-97e7-f75f9aa647b4","version":"2.1.78","gitBranch":"main","slug":"stateful-seeking-melody"}

{"parentUuid":"c1e1204e-df7b-4ca3-bf6d-e58bbd4a5c99","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"616419db-f8ea-4cbf-8b1b-8e3d3d602562","agentId":"a7319d0ecd9725385","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_019UGT7Reza2gZ4Q1mQQhznN","type":"tool_result","content":"     1→---\n     2→type: player-note\n     3→chapter: 71\n     4→author: \"Brigit\"\n     5→title: \"The Mages Tournament\"\n     6→group: \"T\"\n     7→source: [\"NFS/Mardov Dnd Reference Guide/Chapter 71T - Brigit_s Notes - The Mages Tournament.docx\"]\n     8→---\n     9→\n    10→# Chapter 71T: The Mages Tournament\n    11→\n    12→*Notes by Brigit*\n    13→\n    14→- TLDR\n    15→\n    16→  - We dealt with the aftermath of dispatching the 6 fingered necromancer\n    17→\n    18→  - We brought back Iris Westrun with Reincarnation\n    19→\n    20→    - And now this small child is a fully grown adult elf\n    21→\n    22→  - Some of the party went down a well and almost got eaten\n    23→\n    24→  - We met up with the Farmer\\'s Daughters, who were collecting cuttings from the Windmill Willow. Somehow these cuttings are able to make mini-eyes of the storm and can be used to make the land habitable\n    25→\n    26→  - Fen took a spirit from the necromancer\n    27→\n    28→  - Randall turing tested Scamp\n    29→\n    30→  - We saw the orphanage and gave Iris\\' cutting to her instead of a jackass yanti nobleman.\n    31→\n    32→  - Fen encouraged the Yanti to come to the Distillery\n    33→\n    34→  - We walked through the tree into Faughart\n    35→\n    36→  - We came across Sam and Yvonne and two children of the forest in addition to a strange stag spirit. Iris will now reside and learn from Yvonne\n    37→\n    38→  - Fen found a demiplane and was able to speak with Mahen.\n    39→\n    40→  - Varis was hailed by the guards of his keep - and met their master for the first time. He talked with Kasim about some occult items changing since the death of the necromancer\n    41→\n    42→  - Randall and Knob talked, and Randall offered to sponsor Knob\\'s mechanical turtle workshop. And Knob is going to give Randall a vest with wings on it\n    43→\n    44→  - Sprocket talked to his centaur and discovered that he knew the different elements were rebelling against Mardov - in addition to the exact locations of them.\n    45→\n    46→  - Sprocket also looked in on his clone, who had grown an extra digit. But he removed the extra finger.\n    47→\n    48→  - Once again, his broken pocket watch is ticking\n    49→\n    50→- The Distillery! - Faughart\n    51→\n    52→  - Deep in the forest, Brigit heads to where she saw the stag\n    53→\n    54→  - The moon is overcast, but the forest is alive with twinkling lights.\n    55→\n    56→  - She gets back to the edge of the clearing\n    57→\n    58→  - It stares at her, despite her creeping, waiting for something\n    59→\n    60→  - Brigit assumes the stags form with the locket on display\n    61→\n    62→  - It runs straight at her and charges, connecting and dissolving within her\n    63→\n    64→  - She hears a voice - death comes, I come with a warning. A being that assumes my power moves in this direction. I applaud what you did to the necromancer. We must protect 4 spirits. We carry 1. We need to go retrieve my spirit near the Eavallan Forest. He can guide me there. More children of the forest will come and when 12 arrive, you will know your purpose.\n    65→\n    66→  - A woman walks out of Brigit - Sylvia.\n    67→\n    68→    - Sylvia is looking for Yvonne and Brigit takes her there - pointing out her favorite plants the whole time\n    69→\n    70→- To the Keep\n    71→\n    72→  - Kasim doesn\\'t allow Varis in the room. He needs Kasim\\'s invitation to enter. That\\'s weird.\n    73→\n    74→  - Varis steps in, the purple light flares and fades.\n    75→\n    76→  - Has Varis gone native?\n    77→\n    78→    - Kasim\\'s maps changed - it\\'s not Vodram, It\\'s Mardov.\n    79→\n    80→    - Varis walks out of the room and there are 8 lights on the table, but there\\'s only 4 when Varis entered.\n    81→\n    82→    - He walks in again, the lights flicker again. With just one in the Magister\\'s Circle\n    83→\n    84→  - In the ritual - we got 8 lights in his ritual? Does that relate.\n    85→\n    86→  - One more time - hop out, 8 lights in a wheel sort of shape, hop in, and there\\'s a light on Argos. But just the one in the Magister\\'s Circle is on.\n    87→\n    88→  - First thing in the morning, Varis will check in with Sprocket\n    89→\n    90→- The Temple to the Zodiac\n    91→\n    92→  - Fen listens to Mahen tell story after story\n    93→\n    94→  - Her imagery is still out of place - as if she\\'s not quite sure how to use words to describe images\n    95→\n    96→  - Inyada - his yanti name.\n    97→\n    98→  - A huge flash of lightning. {"parentUuid":"2a321a24-29e8-4ba1-930b-00c2459ca5e9","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"616419db-f8ea-4cbf-8b1b-8e3d3d602562","agentId":"a7319d0ecd9725385","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01CqQjDYYXzjEHh7SiEh4Bi8","type":"tool_result","content":"     1→---\n     2→type: player-note\n     3→chapter: 69\n     4→author: \"Brigit\"\n     5→title: \"The 6 Finger of Life\"\n     6→group: \"T\"\n     7→source: [\"NFS/Mardov Dnd Reference Guide/Chapter 69T - Brigit_s Notes - The 6 Finger of Life.docx\"]\n     8→---\n     9→\n    10→# Chapter 69T: The 6 Finger of Life\n    11→\n    12→*Notes by Brigit*\n    13→\n    14→- TLDR\n    15→\n    16→  - We left the Windmill Willow, had a harrowing experience with Tygras Firebrand, one of the progenitors. We killed him last time, but he has somehow been reborn (due to his fiendness).\n    17→\n    18→  - We had some issues getting familiars back with the party due to the storm of Tempest being released.\n    19→\n    20→- Outside the Traveler\\'s Pony\n    21→\n    22→  - (Going back to the height of the festival)\n    23→\n    24→  - We enter on Knob - a gnomish artificer with a racoon mecha-companion, Scamp. He\\'s come to the festival to scour through garbage to further his inventions.\n    25→\n    26→  - He\\'s heard rumors that The Pegasus\\' group stayed in the third floor and they were tinkering all night long.\n    27→\n    28→  - Knob throws up his hood of elven kind and tries to stay undetected - successfully.\n    29→\n    30→  - And they head to the eastern side of the building, to where they can get in undetected.\n    31→\n    32→  - He peeks into the second floor - mostly abandoned due to people leaving the city. And downstairs there\\'s the sound of something breaking.\n    33→\n    34→  - The third floor - Knob uses his thieves\\' tools to open up the door. There should have been a fireworks show by now, but a huge gust of wind from an explosion, it seems, rocks the inn instead.\n    35→\n    36→  - He\\'s in a very fine room - a room made up for a dignitary - very nice linens, and flowers with a card (apologizing for how check-in was, and offering a free night)\n    37→\n    38→  - The walls begin to shake violently - like in a hurricane\n    39→\n    40→    - Scamp went a little lockjaw, but he seems okay. Except that it seems like his memory has been erased, he doesn't recognize knob and is speaking very differently than before.\n    41→\n    42→    - He is supposed to be a human. His name is Rya.\n    43→\n    44→    - Scamp is fine, but Rya is taking refuge in this vessel for now.\n    45→\n    46→    - Rya believes they\\'re in grave danger, and they need to find somewhere safe from the wind. Aka not here.\n    47→\n    48→  - Knob unlocks a briefcase and takes a look at photos and pink crystal; it\\'s some kind of device but nothing like Knob has seen before.\n    49→\n    50→  - Knob takes everything and puts it in not-Scamps back, who is now walking on two legs instead of the usual 4\n    51→\n    52→  - Knob jumps on his mecha and rides out the way they came.\n    53→\n    54→  - Back outside - the bottom stories are now burning.\n    55→\n    56→  - But he does see a strange 8ft blue dragonborn creature\n    57→\n    58→- A sunny glade\n    59→\n    60→  - We march forward led by a white spirit where Irethia used to be\n    61→\n    62→  - The rangers note that the spirits around them look like Mahen\n    63→\n    64→  - And we head upwards through some pouring rain\n    65→\n    66→- A strange land\n    67→\n    68→  - A young woman steps forward in a heavenly white light - asking for the knight of the dusken wood.\n    69→\n    70→  - Varis steps forward and she bows to him, trying to determine his intentions\n    71→\n    72→    - Varis is wary from the fight, but not unwelcoming entirely\n    73→\n    74→  - She is the messenger and third ally.\n    75→\n    76→  - The message: This petal is in grave danger. This encounter is one of fate. It is one of twin souls. With another, Ned Zalock (6 fingered man). Those who seek to bring about Gardamon is because the defenses are weakening. We need to find the identities of the other twin souls before it is too late. They\\'re using the spirits of the 7 heavens to intertwin twin souls. And there are some people who were created out of experimentation who are unnatural and unholy.\n    77→\n    78→  - The granting of this necromancer\\'s power is dangerous\n    79→\n    80→  - She does know that you can twin souls where twin souls don\\'t exist. But she doesn\\'t know what happens if you twin with someone already twinned.\n    81→\n    82→  - Her kind were transported to the fey wilde and now there\\'s a momentary gap due to the harvest zenith opening.\n    83→\n    84→  - She\\'s attempting to put it back together.\n    85→\n    86→  - This is all related to Varis\\' wish, and because fate allowed Leonidas to do his stuff\n    87→\n    88→  - The final component the necromancer needs is 12 of her kind.\n    89→\n    90→    - He has the ability to create fake vessels for them, traps. And she worries that now that the veil is weakened, he\\'ll act soon.\n    91→\n    92→  - Irethia was the first of their kind to escape, but that was a big exception\n    93→\n    94→  - 4 of her kind have been taken and turned - Randall met them.\n    95→\n    96→    - Whatever has been done to them has changed them and not for the better.\n    97→\n    98→  - This is probably related to the drab - the ones without souls.\n    99→\n   100→  - He\\'s in one of the farms outside the city - creating effigies. We have to burn the effigies. He cannot collect these 12 vessels.\n   101→\n   102→  - The moment is fleeting - maybe 10 more minutes\n   103→\n   104→    - Leonidas\\' power is fleeting\n   105→\n   106→  - But when this is done, we\\'ll be put at the farm\n   107→\n   108→  - Real quick - let\\'s deal with spell slots\n   109→\n   110→    - Fen and Sprocket munch down spell slot candies\n   111→\n   112→  - Thousands of spells have been cast, the corpseflowers have been captured and the effigies have been created\n   113→\n   114→- Back to Knob\n   115→\n   116→  - He\\'s headed to one of the farms, taking damage from the gusts of wind\n   117→\n   118→  - Something seems to have been dragged across the ground\n   119→\n   120→  - Something inanimate made it, and not-Scamp points out fresh, non-human tracks\n   121→\n   122→  - He comes across some people with a mark on their shoulder, marking them for something. They\\'re dragging a large stag carving through the dirt.\n   123→\n   124→    - The wooden carving has maybe something to do with a ritual, but Knob doesn\\'t know for sure\n   125→\n   126→  - Knob slinks into the shadows and just watches from afar.\n   127→\n   128→  - They\\'ve dragged these carvings through the wind storm, and the stag is the last one that needs to go into place\n   129→\n   130→- To the scarecrow-body farm\n   131→\n   132→  - The bodies are covered in flowers, and in the center of the area is a circular-ritual thing. A cloaked figure is near the circle, and begins speaking with the two carrying the large stag sculpture - the stag being place at 12.\n   133→\n   134→  - The two will be rewarded handsomely for their help and their dark signs will be removed.\n   135→\n   136→  - A three headed skeleton picks up the stag and places it exactly where it needs to be.\n   137→\n   138→  - And then the main dude compares himself to a god, and them to dogs. Lovely.\n   139→\n   140→    - Their sign burns with a blue light.\n   141→\n   142→  - The time is almost here.\n   143→\n   144→  - One by one, blue lights appear on the outside of the circle - 12 of them in a clock formation.\n   145→\n   146→  - And a portal appears in the circle and our heroes emerge!\n   147→\n   148→- The ritual and The necromancer\n   149→\n   150→  - He welcomes us with open arms\n   151→\n   152→  - Sprocket doesn\\'t know why, but he feels some magnetism, or some form of power surge to his spellbook.\n   153→\n   154→  - Mehen sees the necromancer, sees his immense power and that he uses it poorly. And that he\\'s getting instructions from a master-unseen.\n   155→\n   156→    - An image of a wolf-alpha chewing a bone\n   157→\n   158→  - He bows showing his left hand that doesn\\'t seem like his own - the 6 fingered hand\n   159→\n   160→  - LETS DO THIS\n   161→\n   162→  - A large club slams behind us as a 3 headed skeleton appears behind us\n   163→\n   164→- ROLL FOR INITIATIVE\n   165→\n   166→  - Fen shoots at the skeleton and hits!\n   167→\n   168→  - Mehen flies 60 ft up, and then heads right\n   169→\n   170→  - 5 skeleton undead creatures appear from around us.\n   171→\n   172→  - Varis flies up 10 ft in the air, and shoots some skeletons\n   173→\n   174→    - The skeletons break apart, but they\\'ll probably come back later\n   175→\n   176→    - And then also attacks the necromancer\n   177→\n   178→  - The necromancer takes another legendary action, reaches out with his hand and performs Withering Touch on a scarecrow. Upon his touch, the scarecrow glows with an blue, and then it fades away\n   179→\n   180→    - The blue light fades over to the portal\n   181→\n   182→    - Note: this is the light of the Pangolin Zodiac\n   183→\n   184→  - The 3 headed skeleton - Volstead - creates a 20 foot wide cloudkill. A poisonous cloud.\n   185→\n   186→    - And then heads to Fen and attacks. CRIT MISS\n   187→\n   188→  - Brigit takes poison damage, turns into a fire elemental, burns the skeleton she passes down and then burns one of the effigies - burning, not down yet. And then she hears screaming in her head.\n   189→\n   190→  - Sprocket takes poison damage, and then looks around with his ethereal eye with 10 spirits coming out of the portal. They are being drawn to the effigies.\n   191→\n   192→    - Heads up north and hides behind a fence and then firebolts the northern most skeleton\n   193→\n   194→  - Volstead takes a legendary action and rushes over and casts a chilling touch on the northern burning effigy and tries to put out the fire\n   195→\n   196→  - One skeleton heads over to attack Randall and fails\n   197→\n   198→  - Knob jumps onto not-Scamp and casts Haste. While he doesn\\'t fully understand what\\'s going on, not-Scamp seems to have a certain understanding and is saying a prayer with every passing scarecrow\n   199→\n   200→  - Scamp dodges\n   201→\n   202→  - A skeleton shifts over to attack Fen, misses. Again, and CRITS.\n   203→\n   204→  - Volstead takes his legendary action and attacks, hitting Fen barely\n   205→\n   206→  - Randall takes damage from poison and does the dance of life with some tree branches. Then disengages from the skeleton. He uses the branch to guide Sprocket\\'s fireball\n   207→\n   208→  - One of the two lackeys fires at Varis, but misses\n   209→\n   210→    - And then rushes toward Brigit\n   211→\n   212→  - The necromancer takes movement south and throws up a Bigsby\\'s Hand - with 6 fingers. It moves over to pick up a skeleton and goes to bring it back to the necromancer\n   213→\n   214→  - The other lackey tries to hit Varis and misses\n   215→\n   216→  - Fen disengages and runs behind the mounted racoon-riding gnome\n   217→\n   218→    - And tells him to start blasting the skeleton\n   219→\n   220→  - Varis hits the necromancer! The harvest festival was only a few hours ago. So we should probably destroy it. It would take a day to get back to the distillery, and a day more to get to the magister\\'s circle.\n    43→\n    44→  - Fen takes a look at the portal, there\\'s a pathway at the bottom of the well that leads somewhere\n    45→\n    46→  - Sprocket identifies the staff of Skulllord - a staff of control undead\n    47→\n    48→- Search the house\n    49→\n    50→  - Balto\\'s body there. Part of the house collapsed on top of him, so we have to shift some things\n    51→\n    52→  - Brigit studies his body to be able to turn into him sometime in the future.\n    53→\n    54→  - Moving Balto\\'s body, there was something he was guarding - a small scroll that is a letter from Iris to Fovis.\n    55→\n    56→    - She needed more help and hoped that he could send some money to help.\n    57→\n    58→  - Randall reincarnates Iris into a half-elf\n    59→\n    60→  - Brigit tries to send out an Animal Messenger to see if there\\'s a wolf pack nearby, maybe we can find her a new puppy\n    61→\n    62→- Down the well\n    63→\n    64→  - Fen and Sprocket head down the well to see what\\'s up\n    65→\n    66→  - Sprocket rides on Fen\\'s shoulder\n    67→\n    68→  - It gets darker and darker and Sprocket casts Dancing Lights\n    69→\n    70→  - They get far and then see some eyes in front of them\n    71→\n    72→  - Sprocket hops onto his broom and gets back to the surface with Mahen\n    73→\n    74→  - Fen sprints back, gets partway back\n    75→\n    76→- Inside the house\n    77→\n    78→  - Varis and Brigit take a look at the terrarium\n    79→\n    80→  - It\\'s a shrine, up on an altar"}]},"uuid":"fca29201-22e1-443b-8df5-749b6bff1a29","timestamp":"2026-03-19T06:00:15.877Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"1813ebab-938f-4e4d-8488-1ad140fde121","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/eviemini/Documents/code/server-manager","sessionId":"e89c0432-c0b0-471a-97e7-f75f9aa647b4","version":"2.1.78","gitBranch":"main","slug":"stateful-seeking-melody"}

{"parentUuid":"816556a2-9fbf-426b-ac6f-2af03960f26e","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"616419db-f8ea-4cbf-8b1b-8e3d3d602562","agentId":"a7319d0ecd9725385","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01D6bgZMdVJzCMnyY2t8KoJp","type":"tool_result","content":"     1→---\n     2→type: location\n     3→category: plane\n     4→name: Vodram\n     5→parent: Outer Planes\n     6→aliases:\n     7→  - The Lost World\n     8→notable_npcs:\n     9→  - \"[[friedrich]]\"\n    10→  - \"[[Nyllia Rend]]\"\n    11→  - \"[[Valdis the Dryad]]\"\n    12→  - \"[[clicker]]\"\n    13→chapters:\n    14→  - Act 3\n    15→source: Places/Outer Planes/Vodram/\n    16→maps: []\n    17→tags:\n    18→  - plane\n    19→  - vodram\n    20→  - feywilde\n    21→  - zodiac\n    22→  - clicker\n    23→---\n    24→\n    25→# Vodram\n    26→\n    27→A lost world existing as an outer plane, Vodram is a realm of ancient ruins, fey magic, and primal forces. It contains its own pocket Feywilde and the Valheim stronghold. Vodram is connected to the Zodiac Locket and the serpent Ananta, and was once home to the Last Curator, Friedrich.\n    28→\n    29→## Planar Characteristics\n    30→\n    31→- **Gravity:** Normal\n    32→- **Light:** Ancient, amber-toned -- like perpetual late afternoon in an old world\n    33→- **Atmosphere:** Haunted by echoes. The plane feels like a memory -- populated by remnants and spectres of what once was.\n    34→\n    35→## Sub-locations\n    36→\n    37→### Valheim\n    38→The stronghold within Vodram.\n    39→- [[friedrich]] -- Last Curator of Vodram\n    40→\n    41→### Abbey of St Ashbringer\n    42→Ancient Vodram temple.\n    43→\n    44→### Vodram's Feywilde\n    45→A pocket Feywilde within Vodram, containing:\n    46→\n    47→**Hengefield:**\n    48→- HengeField stone circle -- Ancient ritual site\n    49→- Pathway from the HengeField\n    50→- [[Yori & Garth]] -- Fey Welcoming Party\n    51→\n    52→**Serpent Meadows:**\n    53→- Fork River\n    54→- Goldenwood Grove -- Sacred grove with return ritual\n    55→- [[Kreena Leafellow]] -- Knight of the Twisting Meadow\n    56→- [[Yarmin and Yenowin]] -- Progenitors\n    57→- Connected to [[ananta]] -- Vodram's Serpent\n    58→\n    59→**Ursa Rock:**\n    60→- Mountains Base -- [[clicker]]'s refuge. Contains Clicker's Gift, Clicker's Story (\"The day the Wolf Fled\"), Farmer Clicker, Ned'Zailok Before, [[Valdis the Dryad]] (Collector of the Lost and Broken)\n    61→- **Usatha's Den** -- The Six Fingered Stone, The Spring of the Forked River (restored and unrestored), [[ursatha]] (the Unmade)\n    62→\n    63→## Key NPCs\n    64→\n    65→- **[[friedrich]]** -- Last Curator of Vodram, keeper of ancient knowledge\n    66→- **[[Nyllia Rend]]** -- Current Owner of the Zodiac Locket\n    67→- **[[Valdis the Dryad]]** -- Collector of the Lost and Broken\n    68→- **[[clicker]]** -- Tabaxi Hunter, took refuge at Mountains Base\n    69→- **[[ursatha]]** -- The Unmade, primordial entity at Usatha's Den\n    70→- **[[Guenivive Lockhardt]]** -- Specter\n    71→\n    72→## History\n    73→\n    74→Vodram represents a world that fell -- its Zenith of Peace suggests it once had its own anchor system that failed or was destroyed. Vodram is connected to the Zodiac Locket and the serpent Ananta, and was once home to the Last Curator, Friedrich.\n    28→\n    29→## Planar Characteristics\n    30→\n    31→- **Gravity:** Normal\n    32→- **Light:** Ancient, amber-toned -- like perpetual late afternoon in an old world\n    33→- **Atmosphere:** Haunted by echoes. {"parentUuid":"b9e3a708-8be5-404a-ba32-4f50e0d1fb73","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"616419db-f8ea-4cbf-8b1b-8e3d3d602562","agentId":"a7319d0ecd9725385","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01YQZCWHS6BWmD3H9WBG6f6n","type":"tool_result","content":"   145→Despite cosmic forces beyond their control, Hiln and Thorkon were able to be together protecting their children and Mardov together\n   146→\n   147→---\n   148→\n   149→### Slide 10\n   150→\n   151→Confluence of Portals, Arrivals and Conflicts of Interest\n   152→\n   153→The missing half of the party walked into the Stone Sanctum via the very Iron Doorways that once held Therzadun! Varis, already aware of the portal, found himself with sensory overload at the sudden weakening of the Autumn Mantle and the arrival of Josh on the plane! Punctuating this, the Bear Zodiac? with the Progenitor, Cycle in tow burst through into the Sanctum and plowed his way into the Mountain’s Zenith. Brigit just managed to rip Cycle from his imprisonment within Shardak, the Bear and place him within the Locket! Fen, last to arrive, tossed the Heart of the Faith to Brigit, mentioned that Josh was at the entrance with the Two Eyed Beholder and claimed that he “got this” before also entering the Zenith. The party decided to help save Josh due to Varis’ connection to him and Bergs pull on the St. Ashbury’s network in that direction!\n   154→\n   155→Reminders:\n   156→\n   157→Varis, Brigit and Fen found Ned Zailok’s old research in the Vodram Feywilde surrounding a fake Zodiac\n   158→\n   159→Ursatha the Unmaid was destroyed in Vodram’s Feywilde, shortly after Mehen was born by bathing her dormant egg in the sweetwater lake the bear was guarding\n   160→\n   161→the statue had the following inscription:\u000b“This haven I give to you, my zodiacs, the pure wild nature, apart from mortal influence, so that you may save worlds that are not your own.”\n   162→\n   163→---\n   164→\n   165→### Slide 11\n   166→\n   167→Vellem the Reforged: Third Time’s the Charm\n   168→\n   169→Josh did his best to survive in a one-on-one against one of the deadliest creatures in the campaign history! Once again, just before all seemed lost, he was rescued by the party, including his commanding officer, Varis! They arrived just in time to see a measly moonbeam unravel a plan centuries in the making! The moonbeam in question, cast on Vellem to burn it in the light, inadvertently revealed the shapeshifted Silence as the Therzadun’s Vessel he was, and thus SHIT GOT REAL when a massive shifting titan made of tadpoles appeared within the beam! Josh needed all the help that Heros of Mardov could provide!\n   170→\n   171→---\n   172→\n   173→### Slide 12\n   174→"}]},"uuid":"47592c74-a0f1-4ffb-953e-830bc6f6926f","timestamp":"2026-03-19T06:00:30.024Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"b9e3a708-8be5-404a-ba32-4f50e0d1fb73","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/eviemini/Documents/code/server-manager","sessionId":"e89c0432-c0b0-471a-97e7-f75f9aa647b4","version":"2.1.78","gitBranch":"main","slug":"stateful-seeking-melody"}

Here is the complete timeline:\n\n---\n\n## Ned'Zailok -- Complete Chronological Timeline\n\n### Identity Summary\n\n**Names:** Ned'Zailok, Ned Zalock, Ned Zailock, \"the 6-fingered Necromancer,\" \"the Necromancer\"\n**Title:** Keeper of Death's Anchor\n**Faction:** Vecna Sworn -- Machu & Death Zenith division (secondary agent under Mordred)\n**Nature:** Lich\n**Signature trait:** A left hand with six fingers that \"doesn't seem like his own\"\n**Location ties:** Tower Bridge, Westrun scarecrow farm (near the Distillery), V-Mardov's Death Zenith, Vodram Feywilde (Ursa Rock)\n**Personal nemesis:** Varis Laindon\n\n---\n\n### Pre-Campaign Era\n\n**~3-4 years before campaign start -- Ned'Zailok's origins and Vecna's service**\n\n- [CANON] Ned'Zailok is a lich serving Vecna. He holds the title \"Keeper of Death's Anchor\" -- the obsidian sword thrust into the Forest's Zenith since time immemorial. His role is to guard the Death's Zenith in V-Mardov, the mirror dimension where Vecna's avatar rules. (Sources: `factions/vecna-sworn.md`, `characters/npcs/organizations/Vecna Sworn.md`)\n\n- [CANON] Vecna \"used\" Ned'Zailok \"for experiments.\" The exact nature of these experiments is unspecified in the vault, but they are connected to the Machu & Death Zenith operations and possibly to the creation of fake zodiac vessels. (Source: `characters/npcs/organizations/Vecna Sworn.md` -- \"Used by Vecna for experiments\")\n\n- [CANON] Ned'Zailok betrayed **Casaval Dekun**, an Eladrin warrior. Casaval was \"born a Machu Elf, now a Fey Construct, the only one of its kind (4 in one)\" -- an Eladrin experiment from Machu. The betrayal's specifics are not detailed, but Casaval later joins the party's allies around Chapter 106+. (Sources: `sources/lore-extraction/intrigue-web.md`, `chapters/act-5/chapter-114.md`)\n\n- [INFERRED] Ned'Zailok conducted research in the **Vodram Feywilde**, specifically at **Ursa Rock** (Mountains Base area). The vault lists a location called \"Ned'Zailok Before\" at Ursa Rock alongside \"The Six Fingered Stone\" at Usatha's Den nearby. This research involved creating a **fake Zodiac** -- a counterfeit zodiac spirit, likely part of Vecna's experiments. (Sources: `locations/planes/vodram.md` line 60-61, `chapters/act-5/chapter-106.md` -- \"Varis, Brigit and Fen found Ned Zailok's old research in the Vodram Feywilde surrounding a fake Zodiac\")\n\n**~2-2.5 years before campaign start -- Attack on Tower Bridge**\n\n- [CANON] Ned'Zailok raises an army of undead -- zombies, skeletons, wraiths, mummies -- and attacks **Tower Bridge**, a wilderness outpost near Dawnpass at the edge of Eavallan Forest where elves and humans live. (Source: `sources/backstories/varys-liadon.md`)\n\n- [CANON] Two weeks before the army arrives, Varis's father **Yeldan Liadon** spots undead scouts. The local lord believes him but cannot convince others in time. (Source: `sources/backstories/varys-liadon.md`)\n\n- [CANON] The army arrives. Varis (age 95, ~17 in human years) and his father volunteer for the militia. They hold for one week. (Source: `sources/backstories/varys-liadon.md`)\n\n- [CANON] Yeldan is sent as the fastest scout to request aid. He never arrives at the lord's seat. **Yeldan goes missing** -- presumed dead but never confirmed. (Source: `sources/backstories/varys-liadon.md`, `lore/timeline.md`)\n\n- [CANON] The militia breaks a day after Yeldan's departure. The **Scarlet Tunic Brigade** arrives -- too late to hold Tower Bridge, but fast enough to cover the evacuation. Most civilians escape. The militia takes massive losses. (Source: `sources/backstories/varys-liadon.md`, `factions/scarlet-tunic-brigade.md`)\n\n- [CANON] STB commander **Dockson** -- an honorable paladin -- shames the local lord into committing resources. (Source: `sources/backstories/varys-liadon.md`)\n\n- [CANON] Varis enlists with the STB. (Source: `sources/backstories/varys-liadon.md`)\n\n**~2.5 years to ~1 year before campaign start -- 18 months of fighting**\n\n- [CANON] The combined STB and militia forces fight Ned'Zailok for **eighteen months** before he is defeated. Most fighters disperse afterward. (Sources: `sources/backstories/varys-liadon.md`, `lore/timeline.md` -- \"Ned'Zailok defeated after 18 months of fighting\")\n\n- [INFERRED] Ned'Zailok's \"defeat\" was not permanent destruction. He is a lich and likely had a clone or phylactery-equivalent mechanism. The vault never describes him as \"destroyed\" -- only \"defeated.\" Varis specifically notes \"Can't ever be sure a lich is gone.\" (Source: `sources/backstories/varys-liadon.md`)\n\n- [SPECULATIVE] During this period, Ned'Zailok may have retreated to V-Mardov or the Vodram Feywilde to continue his role as Keeper of Death's Anchor. His attack on Tower Bridge may itself have been part of Vecna's experiments -- soul harvesting from a military engagement near Eavallan Forest and the Forest's Zenith's sphere of influence.\n\n---\n\n### Campaign Era (Chapters 0-136)\n\n**Chapters 0-63 -- Absence / Operating Off-Screen**\n\n- [INFERRED] Ned'Zailok does not appear directly in these chapters. He is operating in V-Mardov as Keeper of Death's Anchor and conducting research in the Vodram Feywilde (creating fake zodiacs). The party encounters the Forest's Zenith prophecy structure (Chapters 24-25) which foretells they will eventually confront the lich, and Varis keeps an ear out for word of him. (Source: `characters/pcs/Varis Laindon.md` -- \"Always keeps an ear out for word that Ned'Zailok has resurfaced\")\n\n- [CANON] The Chapter 24 Tomb of the Last Curator hieroglyphics for the Forest Room depict: \"A seven-point crown made of sludge\" and other Death Zenith imagery, foreshadowing the confrontation. (Source: `lore/prophecies.md`)\n\n**Chapter 64T -- Prelude (Harvest Festival)**\n\n- [CANON] The Harvest Festival is underway near the Distillery. Varis meets Iris Westrun at the festival -- she runs a flower farm with her dog Balto. Strange things are happening: people are taking flowers, there is a \"curse,\" and Balto has been injured. (Source: `sources/player-notes/ch066T-brigit.md`)\n\n- [INFERRED] Ned'Zailok (or his agents) have been preparing in the Westrun farm area -- creating effigies, capturing corpse flowers, and setting up the zodiac-harvesting ritual.\n\n**Chapter 69T -- \"The 6 Finger of Life\" -- The Scarecrow Farm Battle**\n\n- [CANON] A mysterious messenger appears to Varis in a heavenly glade, addressing him as \"Knight of the Dusken Wood.\" She warns that \"this petal is in grave danger\" and identifies **Ned Zalock, the 6-fingered man.** Those who seek to bring about Gard'Daemon are doing so because \"the defenses are weakening.\" She explains Ned is using spirits of the 7 heavens to intertwine twin souls, and some beings \"were created out of experimentation who are unnatural and unholy.\" (Source: `sources/player-notes/ch069T-brigit.md` line 76)\n\n- [CANON] The messenger warns: **\"The final component the necromancer needs is 12 of her kind.\"** He can create fake vessels (traps/effigies) for them. The veil has weakened and he will act soon. He is at \"one of the farms outside the city -- creating effigies. We have to burn the effigies.\" (Source: `sources/player-notes/ch069T-brigit.md` lines 88-100)\n\n- [CANON] Separately, the artificer **Knob** (with his raccoon mecha-companion Scamp) witnesses people with dark marks dragging a large stag carving to the farm. A cloaked figure oversees the placement of 12 carvings in a clock formation -- zodiac effigies. The figure compares himself to a god and the workers to dogs. (Source: `sources/player-notes/ch069T-brigit.md` lines 130-145)\n\n- [CANON] The party arrives through a portal at the **scarecrow-body farm**. Bodies are covered in flowers. The Necromancer welcomes them. His three-headed skeleton servant **Volstead** accompanies him. Sprocket feels \"some magnetism, or some form of power surge to his spellbook.\" Mehen senses the necromancer's immense power and that \"he's getting instructions from a master-unseen\" (image of a wolf-alpha chewing a bone). (Source: `sources/player-notes/ch069T-brigit.md` lines 148-156)\n\n- [CANON] **\"He bows showing his left hand that doesn't seem like his own -- the 6 fingered hand.\"** (Source: `sources/player-notes/ch069T-brigit.md` line 158)\n\n- [CANON] Major battle ensues. The Necromancer uses **Withering Touch** on effigies to harvest zodiac spirits (Pangolin, Owl, Snake identified). He uses **Bigsby's Hand with 6 fingers**, **Finger of Death** (dropping Varis twice, Fen, Sprocket, and Randall), and **Circle of Death**. His skeleton Volstead uses Cloudkill and Chilling Touch. (Source: `sources/player-notes/ch069T-brigit.md`)\n\n- [CANON] **Varis hits a 77-damage critical** with his lightning bowstring. The necromancer points at Varis: \"YOU.\" He seems to almost recognize Varis. (Source: `sources/player-notes/ch069T-brigit.md` line 332-336)\n\n- [CANON] **Varis's vision while dying:** He sees himself in a tower with the necromancer, sitting by a fire. The necromancer thanks Varis for joining him. A spirit with a crown of stag horns enters. The necromancer bows and is absorbed. (Source: `sources/player-notes/ch069T-brigit.md` lines 374-382)\n\n- [CANON] **Fen delivers the killing blow** -- an arrow through the 6-fingered hand and then through his chest. The necromancer falls. (Source: `sources/player-notes/ch069T-brigit.md` lines 462-464)\n\n- [CANON] When he falls, he addresses Sprocket: \"I know death is not the end for you, you're welcome.\" (Source: `sources/player-notes/ch069T-brigit.md` line 408)\n\n- [CANON] **Ned collected 8 of the 12 zodiac spirits** before being stopped. The party burned several effigies and Fen captured one spirit into his ring. (Source: `sources/player-notes/ch069T-brigit.md`)\n\n**Chapter 70T -- Aftermath**\n\n- [CANON] Ned'Zailok's body turns to dust. Varis finds two items: a **black cloak** (Cloak of the Arch Magi, evil-aligned) and a **symbol** -- a crown with 12 points, 8 of them lit (corresponding to the 8 spirits he harvested). The symbol has a teleportation circle inscribed within it and is under transpositional magic -- it exists in multiple locations and may be being tracked. (Source: `sources/player-notes/ch070T-brigit.md`)\n\n- [CANON] Sprocket suspects Ned has a **clone** somewhere and may already be back to full strength and tracking them via the symbol. (Source: `sources/player-notes/ch070T-brigit.md` line 42)\n\n- [CANON] The party finds a **Staff of the Skull Lord** (Staff of Control Undead) and discovers Iris Westrun dead (killed by Volstead during the battle). Randall reincarnates Iris as a half-elf. (Source: `sources/player-notes/ch070T-brigit.md`)\n\n**Chapter 71T -- Aftermath continues**\n\n- [CANON] The party deals with the aftermath. Sprocket's clone has \"grown an extra digit\" (6th finger) -- Sprocket removes it. His spellbook's hand mark \"moved from the right page to the left and gained a finger.\" The necromancer's influence is literally leaking into Sprocket. (Sources: `sources/player-notes/ch071T-brigit.md` lines 46-47, `sources/player-notes/ch070T-brigit.md` line 31)\n\n- [CANON] The stag spirit that entered during the battle speaks to Brigit: \"Death comes, I come with a warning. A being that assumes my power moves in this direction.\" The spirit applauds what the party did to the necromancer and says they must protect 4 zodiac spirits -- they carry 1 and need to retrieve another near Eavallan Forest. (Source: `sources/player-notes/ch071T-brigit.md` line 64)\n\n- [CANON] Sprocket checks with Lillith about \"6-fingers or necromancy.\" There is a **secret necromancy track** in the Magister's school. (Source: `sources/player-notes/ch071T-brigit.md` line 224)\n\n- [CANON] Reynardine appears at the Mages Tournament with a **new 6th digit**. He warns that one of the nominees is an imposter and a member of \"the necromancers covenant.\" He claims to be the \"official nominee for the necromancers\" and resigns, then flies away as a crow. (Source: `sources/player-notes/ch071T-brigit.md` lines 324-332)\n\n- [INFERRED] Reynardine's 6th finger and connection to the \"necromancers covenant\" suggests the 6-finger motif is not unique to Ned but is a mark of a broader necromantic order connected to Vecna's operations.\n\n**Chapter 88 -- Westrun fields referenced**\n\n- [CANON] The party is questioned about \"how they knew to be at the Westrun fields\" -- the site of the scarecrow farm battle. This suggests the location had strategic importance beyond the immediate encounter. (Source: `sources/player-notes/ch088-brigit.md` line 100)\n\n**Chapter 106 -- Ned's Vodram research discovered**\n\n- [CANON] A slide presentation reminds the party: **\"Varis, Brigit and Fen found Ned Zailok's old research in the Vodram Feywilde surrounding a fake Zodiac.\"** This confirms Ned was operating in Vodram (the lost world / outer plane), conducting zodiac-related experiments before the scarecrow farm attack. (Source: `chapters/act-5/chapter-106.md` line 157)\n\n- [CANON] Connected detail: **Ursatha the Unmade** was destroyed in Vodram's Feywilde shortly after Mehen was born, at the same location (Ursa Rock) where Ned's research was found. (Source: `chapters/act-5/chapter-106.md` line 159)\n\n---\n\n### The Planned Endgame (DM Notes -- Not Yet Played)\n\n- [CANON -- DM PLAN] The Forest's Zenith arc climaxes with Mordred using the Heart of the Wilde to awaken the Forest Guardian (the big boss). After the party defeats the Forest Guardian, the Heart of the Wilde **transports the ENTIRE party to V-Mardov -- inside the Death's Zenith** -- where they fight **Lich Ned'Zailok, Keeper of Death's Anchor**, and steal the Anchor of Death. (Source: `sources/dm-notes/Zodiacs and Player Symbolism.md` line 14, `sources/lore-extraction/story-arc-dm-notes.md` line 99)\n\n- [CANON -- DM PLAN] **Varis** is cosmically linked to Ned'Zailok and the Death Zenith. He is destined to \"sit in the control room for the Forest's Zenith at the final fight\" (Gard'Daemon). (Source: `sources/dm-notes/Zodiacs and Player Symbolism.md` line 14)\n\n- [CANON -- DM PLAN] The mechanic: \"Heart of the Wilde on our side, Anchor of Death on their side.\" The Forest's Zenith in Mardov and the Death's Zenith in V-Mardov are mirrors. (Source: `sources/dm-notes/Zodiacs and Player Symbolism.md` line 14)\n\n---\n\n### Ned'Zailok's Relationship to Vecna's Plan\n\n- [CANON] Ned is a **secondary agent** in the Zenith of Death operation. Mordred is the primary agent. Ned operates in V-Mardov guarding the Death's Zenith and the Anchor of Death, while Mordred operates in Mardov at the Forest's Zenith. (Source: `factions/vecna-sworn.md`)\n\n- [CANON] Vecna used Ned for \"experiments.\" Combined with the fake zodiac research in Vodram and the 69T zodiac-harvesting ritual, these experiments likely involve creating counterfeit zodiac vessels and soul-twinning -- artificially creating twin-soul bonds using spirits of the 7 heavens. (Sources: multiple)\n\n- [INFERRED] Ned's attack on Tower Bridge may have been strategic rather than arbitrary -- Tower Bridge sits near Dawnpass and Eavallan Forest, close to the Forest's Zenith's sphere of influence. The 18-month campaign could have been a massive soul-harvesting operation (dead soldiers = undead army fuel) and/or a probe of the Forest Zenith's defenses.\n\n- [SPECULATIVE] The vision Varis sees of sitting peacefully in a tower with the Necromancer by a fire, and the stag spirit entering to absorb the necromancer, may foreshadow the endgame resolution: Varis in the Forest's Zenith control room, the zodiac spirits reclaiming what Ned stole, and the necromancer's power being subsumed back into the natural order.\n\n---\n\n### What Was He Doing Between Appearances?\n\n| Period | Activity | Confidence |\n|--------|----------|------------|\n| Pre-Tower Bridge | Research in Vodram Feywilde (Ursa Rock) -- creating fake zodiacs, conducting Vecna's experiments | [CANON] |\n| Tower Bridge campaign | Leading undead army near Forest's Zenith territory for ~18 months | [CANON] |\n| Post-\"defeat\" to Ch 64T | Guarding Death's Zenith in V-Mardov; preparing Westrun farm ritual | [INFERRED] |\n| Ch 64T-69T | Operating at the Westrun scarecrow farm, creating zodiac effigies, collecting corpse flowers, marking collaborators with dark signs | [CANON] |\n| Post-Ch 69T death | Body turned to dust; likely resurrected via clone (Sprocket's suspicion).
